from module import finMind as fm, futures_utils as FU
from common import db
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

import pandas as pd
import traceback

# =========================================================
# 抓取期貨、選擇權近期資料
# =========================================================
def run_tx_txo_all(
    start_date="2020-01-01",
    end_date=None,
):
    """
    抓 TX + TXO 全資料
    """

    if isinstance(start_date, str):
        start_date = pd.to_datetime(start_date)

    if end_date is None:
        end_date = datetime.today()
    elif isinstance(end_date, str):
        end_date = pd.to_datetime(end_date)

    futures_id = "TX"
    option_id = "TXO"

    results = {}

    jobs = [
        {
            "name": "futures_daily",
            "func": lambda: fm.get_tw_futures_daily(
                futures_id=futures_id,
                start_date=start_date,
                end_date=end_date,
            ),
        },
        {
            "name": "futures_final_settlement_price",
            "func": lambda: fm.get_tw_futures_final_settlement_price(
                futures_id=futures_id,
                start_date=start_date,
                end_date=end_date,
            ),
        },
        {
            "name": "futures_institutional_investors_after_hours",
            "func": lambda: fm.get_tw_futures_institutional_investors_after_hours(
                futures_id=futures_id,
                start_date=start_date,
                end_date=end_date,
            ),
        },
        {
            "name": "option_final_settlement_price",
            "func": lambda: fm.get_tw_option_final_settlement_price(
                option_id=option_id,
                start_date=start_date,
                end_date=end_date,
            ),
        },
    ]

    print("======================================")
    print("開始抓取 FinMind TX / TXO 資料")
    print(f"區間: {start_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')} ~ {end_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}")
    print("======================================")

    for job in jobs:
        job_name = job["name"]
        print(f"\n>>> 開始執行：{job_name}")

        try:
            df = job["func"]()

            if df is None:
                df = pd.DataFrame()

            results[job_name] = df
            print(f"<<< 完成：{job_name}，筆數 = {len(df)}")

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"!!! 失敗：{job_name}")
            print(f"錯誤訊息：{e}")
            traceback.print_exc()
            results[job_name] = pd.DataFrame()

    print("\n======================================")
    print("全部任務執行完畢，結果摘要：")
    print("======================================")
    for k, v in results.items():
        print(f"{k}: {len(v)} 筆")

    return results

# =========================================================
# 主函式
# =========================================================
def build_futures_report_daily_raw(
    start_date,
    end_date=None,
    futures_id="TX",
    recent_rebuild_days=0,
    force_update=False,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    target_table = "futures_report_daily_raw"
    idx_key = str(futures_id)

    start_ts = FU._to_ts(start_date)
    end_ts = FU._to_ts(end_date if end_date is not None else datetime.today())

    if start_ts > end_ts:
        raise ValueError("start_date 不可大於 end_date")

    # === 更新來源資料 ===
    fm.get_tw_futures_daily(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        start_date=start_ts,
        end_date=end_ts,
    )
    fm.get_tw_futures_final_settlement_price(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        start_date=start_ts,
        end_date=end_ts,
    )
    fm.get_tw_futures_institutional_investors(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        start_date=start_ts,
        end_date=end_ts,
    )
    fm.get_tw_futures_institutional_investors_after_hours(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        start_date=start_ts,
        end_date=end_ts,
    )
    
    # === 建立價格對照表 ===
    weighted_close_map, tx_close_map, tx_settlement_map = FU._build_market_price_maps(
        start_ts=start_ts,
        end_ts=end_ts,
        futures_id=futures_id,
    )

    # === 查快取 ===
    span_row = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT start_date, end_date
        FROM date_span
        WHERE target_table = ? AND idx_key = ?
        """,
        (target_table, idx_key),
    )

    mem_s = (
        pd.Timestamp(span_row.loc[0, "start_date"]).normalize()
        if not span_row.empty and pd.notna(span_row.loc[0, "start_date"])
        else None
    )
    mem_e = (
        pd.Timestamp(span_row.loc[0, "end_date"]).normalize()
        if not span_row.empty and pd.notna(span_row.loc[0, "end_date"])
        else None
    )

    actual_cache_row = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT MIN(日期) AS min_date, MAX(日期) AS max_date
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
        """,
        (idx_key,),
    )

    actual_mem_s = (
        pd.Timestamp(actual_cache_row.loc[0, "min_date"]).normalize()
        if not actual_cache_row.empty and pd.notna(actual_cache_row.loc[0, "min_date"])
        else None
    )
    actual_mem_e = (
        pd.Timestamp(actual_cache_row.loc[0, "max_date"]).normalize()
        if not actual_cache_row.empty and pd.notna(actual_cache_row.loc[0, "max_date"])
        else None
    )

    if mem_s is not None and actual_mem_s is not None:
        mem_s = max(mem_s, actual_mem_s)

    if mem_e is not None and actual_mem_e is not None:
        mem_e = min(mem_e, actual_mem_e)

    fetch_ranges: list[tuple[pd.Timestamp, pd.Timestamp]] = []

    if force_update:
        fetch_ranges = [(start_ts, end_ts)]
    else:
        if mem_s is None or mem_e is None:
            fetch_ranges = [(start_ts, end_ts)]
        else:
            if start_ts < mem_s:
                fetch_ranges.append((start_ts, mem_s - pd.Timedelta(days=1)))
            if end_ts > mem_e:
                fetch_ranges.append((mem_e + pd.Timedelta(days=1), end_ts))

    print(
        f"[build] {idx_key} "
        f"req=({FU._dstr(start_ts)}~{FU._dstr(end_ts)}) "
        f"cache=({FU._dstr(mem_s) if mem_s is not None else None}~{FU._dstr(mem_e) if mem_e is not None else None}) "
        f"ranges={[( FU._dstr(s), FU._dstr(e) ) for s, e in fetch_ranges]}"
    )

    upsert_sql = """
    INSERT INTO futures_report_daily_raw (
        futures_id,
        日期, 年, 月, 星期, 週次, 天別,
        日盤開, 日盤收, 日盤高, 日盤低,
        日漲跌, 日振幅, 日振幅開盤比, 日振幅昨收比, 日收減日開, 日收開昨收比,
        夜盤開, 夜盤收, 夜盤高, 夜盤低,
        日法人多倉, 日法人空倉, 夜法人多單量, 夜法人空單量,
        日成交量, 夜成交量,
        日5MA, 日10MA, 日20MA, 日60MA, 日120MA,
        夜5MA, 夜10MA, 夜20MA, 夜60MA, 夜120MA,
        加權收盤價, 大台收盤價, 大台結算價
    )
    VALUES (
        ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?
    )
    ON CONFLICT(futures_id, 日期) DO UPDATE SET
        年 = excluded.年,
        月 = excluded.月,
        星期 = excluded.星期,
        週次 = excluded.週次,
        天別 = excluded.天別,
        日盤開 = excluded.日盤開,
        日盤收 = excluded.日盤收,
        日盤高 = excluded.日盤高,
        日盤低 = excluded.日盤低,
        日漲跌 = excluded.日漲跌,
        日振幅 = excluded.日振幅,
        日振幅開盤比 = excluded.日振幅開盤比,
        日振幅昨收比 = excluded.日振幅昨收比,
        日收減日開 = excluded.日收減日開,
        日收開昨收比 = excluded.日收開昨收比,
        夜盤開 = excluded.夜盤開,
        夜盤收 = excluded.夜盤收,
        夜盤高 = excluded.夜盤高,
        夜盤低 = excluded.夜盤低,
        日法人多倉 = excluded.日法人多倉,
        日法人空倉 = excluded.日法人空倉,
        夜法人多單量 = excluded.夜法人多單量,
        夜法人空單量 = excluded.夜法人空單量,
        日成交量 = excluded.日成交量,
        夜成交量 = excluded.夜成交量,
        加權收盤價 = excluded.加權收盤價,
        大台收盤價 = excluded.大台收盤價,
        大台結算價 = excluded.大台結算價
    """

    rows = []
    dates_to_rebuild = set()

    for fs, fe in fetch_ranges:
        df_dates = db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT DISTINCT date
            FROM fm_taiwan_futures_daily
            WHERE futures_id = ?
              AND trading_session = 'position'
              AND date >= ?
              AND date <= ?
            ORDER BY date
            """,
            (idx_key, FU._dstr(fs), FU._dstr(fe)),
        )

        if df_dates is not None and not df_dates.empty:
            dates_to_rebuild.update(
                pd.to_datetime(df_dates["date"]).dt.normalize().tolist()
            )

    if not force_update and recent_rebuild_days > 0:
        dates_to_rebuild.update(
            FU._get_recent_trading_dates(recent_rebuild_days, futures_id)
        )

    if not force_update:
        dates_to_rebuild.update(
            FU._get_incomplete_night_dates(start_ts, end_ts, futures_id)
        )

    dates_to_rebuild = sorted(dates_to_rebuild)

    print(
        f"[dates_to_rebuild] count={len(dates_to_rebuild)}, "
        f"tail={[ FU._dstr(x) for x in dates_to_rebuild[-10:] ] if dates_to_rebuild else []}"
    )

    for d in dates_to_rebuild:
        day_row = FU._get_month_contract_row(
            d,
            "position",
            FU._get_front_contract_month(d),
            futures_id,
        )
        if day_row is None:
            print(f"[skip] {idx_key} {FU._dstr(d)} day_row is None")
            continue

        next_d = FU._get_next_after_market_date(d, futures_id)
        night_row = None
        if next_d is not None:
            night_row = FU._get_month_contract_row(
                next_d,
                "after_market",
                FU._get_front_contract_month(next_d),
                futures_id,
            )

        o = float(day_row["open"]) if pd.notna(day_row["open"]) else None
        c = float(day_row["close"]) if pd.notna(day_row["close"]) else None
        h = float(day_row["max"]) if pd.notna(day_row["max"]) else None
        l = float(day_row["min"]) if pd.notna(day_row["min"]) else None

        day_vol = (
            float(day_row["volume"])
            if pd.notna(day_row["volume"])
            else None
        )
        night_vol = (
            float(night_row["volume"])
            if night_row is not None and pd.notna(night_row["volume"])
            else None
        )

        prev_df = db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT 日盤收
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
              AND 日期 < ?
            ORDER BY 日期 DESC
            LIMIT 1
            """,
            (idx_key, FU._dstr(d)),
        )

        prev_close = (
            float(prev_df.loc[0, "日盤收"])
            if not prev_df.empty and pd.notna(prev_df.loc[0, "日盤收"])
            else None
        )

        # === 日漲跌：以昨收為基準 ===
        diff = (
            (c - prev_close)
            if (c is not None and prev_close is not None)
            else None
        )
        amp = (h - l) if (h is not None and l is not None) else None
        amp_open = (amp / o) if (amp is not None and o is not None and o != 0) else None
        amp_prev = (
            (amp / prev_close)
            if (amp is not None and prev_close is not None and prev_close != 0)
            else None
        )

        # 日收減日開 / 日收開昨收比 仍維持收開邏輯
        close_minus_open = (
            (c - o)
            if (c is not None and o is not None)
            else None
        )
        close_open_vs_prev = (
            (close_minus_open / prev_close)
            if (
                close_minus_open is not None
                and prev_close is not None
                and prev_close != 0
            )
            else None
        )

        # === 日法人未平倉 ===
        inst_day_df = db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT
                SUM(COALESCE(long_open_interest_balance_volume, 0)) AS long_oi,
                SUM(COALESCE(short_open_interest_balance_volume, 0)) AS short_oi
            FROM fm_taiwan_futures_institutional_investors
            WHERE data_id = ?
              AND date = ?
            """,
            (idx_key, FU._dstr(d)),
        )

        day_inst_long = (
            float(inst_day_df.loc[0, "long_oi"])
            if not inst_day_df.empty and pd.notna(inst_day_df.loc[0, "long_oi"])
            else None
        )
        day_inst_short = (
            float(inst_day_df.loc[0, "short_oi"])
            if not inst_day_df.empty and pd.notna(inst_day_df.loc[0, "short_oi"])
            else None
        )

        # === 夜法人欄位：用下一交易日夜盤資料 ===
        night_inst_long = None
        night_inst_short = None

        if next_d is not None:
            inst_night_df = db.query_to_df(
                """
                SELECT
                    SUM(COALESCE(long_deal_volume, 0)) AS long_vol,
                    SUM(COALESCE(short_deal_volume, 0)) AS short_vol
                FROM fm_taiwan_futures_institutional_investors_after_hours
                WHERE futures_id = ?
                  AND date = ?
                """,
                (idx_key, FU._dstr(next_d)),
            )

            night_inst_long = (
                float(inst_night_df.loc[0, "long_vol"])
                if not inst_night_df.empty and pd.notna(inst_night_df.loc[0, "long_vol"])
                else None
            )
            night_inst_short = (
                float(inst_night_df.loc[0, "short_vol"])
                if not inst_night_df.empty and pd.notna(inst_night_df.loc[0, "short_vol"])
                else None
            )

        rows.append(
            (
                idx_key,
                FU._dstr(d),
                d.year,
                d.month,
                FU._weekday_text(d),
                FU._week_no(d),
                FU._day_type_text(d),

                o, c, h, l,

                diff,
                amp,
                amp_open,
                amp_prev,
                close_minus_open,
                close_open_vs_prev,

                float(night_row["open"]) if night_row is not None and pd.notna(night_row["open"]) else None,
                float(night_row["close"]) if night_row is not None and pd.notna(night_row["close"]) else None,
                float(night_row["max"]) if night_row is not None and pd.notna(night_row["max"]) else None,
                float(night_row["min"]) if night_row is not None and pd.notna(night_row["min"]) else None,

                day_inst_long,
                day_inst_short,
                night_inst_long,
                night_inst_short,

                day_vol,
                night_vol,

                None, None, None, None, None,
                None, None, None, None, None,
                
                weighted_close_map.get(FU._dstr(d)),
                tx_close_map.get(FU._dstr(d)),
                tx_settlement_map.get(FU._dstr(d)),
            )
        )

    print(f"[rows_to_upsert] count={len(rows)}")
    if rows:
        print(f"[rows_to_upsert] tail_dates={[r[1] for r in rows[-5:]]}")
        db.execute_sql(upsert_sql, rows)

    # === date_span 更新 ===
    span_df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT MIN(日期) AS min_date, MAX(日期) AS max_date
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
        """,
        (idx_key,),
    )

    if not span_df.empty and pd.notna(span_df.loc[0, "max_date"]):
        actual_s = pd.Timestamp(span_df.loc[0, "min_date"]).normalize()
        actual_e = pd.Timestamp(span_df.loc[0, "max_date"]).normalize()

        safe_e = actual_e - pd.Timedelta(days=1)

        if safe_e >= actual_s:
            db.execute_sql(
                """
                INSERT INTO date_span (target_table, idx_key, start_date, end_date, updated_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
                ON CONFLICT(target_table, idx_key) DO UPDATE SET
                    start_date = excluded.start_date,
                    end_date   = excluded.end_date,
                    updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                """,
                (target_table, idx_key, FU._dstr(actual_s), FU._dstr(safe_e)),
            )

    # === 重算 MA ===
    FU._recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_ma(futures_id)
    
    # === 重算 價量 ===
    FU._recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_pv(futures_id)
    
    # === 重算 月選 Call / Put total ===
    FU._recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_month_option(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        option_id="TXO",
        target_dates=dates_to_rebuild,
    )
    
    # === 重算 週三選 Call / Put total ===
    FU._recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_weekly_wed_option(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        option_id="TXO",
        target_dates=dates_to_rebuild,
    )
    
    # === 重算 週五選 Call / Put total ===
    FU._recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_weekly_friday_option(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        option_id="TXO",
        target_dates=dates_to_rebuild,
    )

    # === 回填布林通道 ===
    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT 日期, 日盤收
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (idx_key,),
    )

    if df is not None and not df.empty:
        df["日期"] = pd.to_datetime(df["日期"])
        df = df.sort_values("日期").copy()

        df["ma20"] = df["日盤收"].rolling(window=20, min_periods=20).mean()
        df["std"] = df["日盤收"].rolling(window=20, min_periods=20).std()
        df["upper"] = df["ma20"] + 2 * df["std"]
        df["lower"] = df["ma20"] - 2 * df["std"]

        update_rows = []
        for _, row in df.iterrows():
            update_rows.append(
                (
                    float(row["std"]) if pd.notna(row["std"]) else None,
                    float(row["upper"]) if pd.notna(row["upper"]) else None,
                    float(row["lower"]) if pd.notna(row["lower"]) else None,
                    idx_key,
                    row["日期"].strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
                )
            )

        db.execute_sql(
            """
            UPDATE futures_report_daily_raw
            SET
                日布林標準差 = ?,
                日布林上軌 = ?,
                日布林下軌 = ?
            WHERE futures_id = ?
              AND 日期 = ?
            """,
            update_rows,
        )
        
    backfill_MTX_TMF_retail_long_short_ratio(start_date, end_date)

    return db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT *
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND 日期 >= ?
          AND 日期 <= ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (idx_key, FU._dstr(start_ts), FU._dstr(end_ts)),
    )
    
def sync_reopurt_csv_to_view():
    df = db.sql_to_df("SELECT * FROM v_futures_report_daily")
    
    print(df.head())
    print(df.tail())
    
    drop_cols = [
        "id", "futures_id" #, "created_at"
    ]
    
    output_csv(df, "futures_report_daily.csv", drop_cols)
    
    return df
    
    
def output_csv(
    df,
    csv_dir,
    drop_cols
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    
    if drop_cols:
        df.drop(columns=drop_cols, inplace=True)
    
    if csv_dir == "" or csv_dir is None:
        csv_dir = "output.csv"
    
    df.to_csv(csv_dir, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
    return df

def routine_output():
    where_condition = """
        AND target_table LIKE 'fm_%' 
        AND (target_table LIKE '%_futures_%' OR target_table LIKE '%_option_%')
    """
    db._limit_date_span(where_condition=where_condition)
    sDt = datetime(2024, 1, 1)
    eDt = datetime.now() + timedelta(days=3)

    df = build_futures_report_daily_raw(
        start_date=sDt,
        end_date=eDt,
        futures_id="TX",
        recent_rebuild_days = 5
    )

    sync_reopurt_csv_to_view()

# =========================================================
# 欄位回填函式
# =========================================================
def backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_volume(
    start_date=None,
    end_date=None,
    futures_id="TX",
    only_null=True,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    回填 futures_report_daily_raw 的：
    - 日成交量
    - 夜成交量

    規則：
    - 日成交量：取當天 D 的 position session、近月/月合約 row 的 volume
    - 夜成交量：取下一交易日 N 的 after_market session、近月/月合約 row 的 volume，
      但寫回 D 這一天

    參數：
    - start_date, end_date:
        可指定只補某段日期；若都不給，則補整張 futures_report_daily_raw
    - futures_id:
        預設 TX
    - only_null:
        True  -> 只補 日成交量/夜成交量 任一為 NULL 的列
        False -> 指定區間全部重算覆蓋

    回傳：
    - 補完後的該區間 futures_report_daily_raw 資料
    """

    idx_key = str(futures_id)

    # =========================================================
    # 1. 決定要補哪些日期
    # =========================================================
    where_sql = ["futures_id = ?"]
    params = [idx_key]

    if start_date is not None:
        start_ts = FU._to_ts(start_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 >= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(start_ts))
    else:
        start_ts = None

    if end_date is not None:
        end_ts = FU._to_ts(end_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 <= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(end_ts))
    else:
        end_ts = None

    if only_null:
        where_sql.append("(日成交量 IS NULL OR 夜成交量 IS NULL)")

    target_df = db.query_to_df(
        f"""
        SELECT 日期
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE {' AND '.join(where_sql)}
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        tuple(params),
    )

    if target_df is None or target_df.empty:
        print("[backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_volume] 無需回填資料")
        if start_ts is None or end_ts is None:
            return db.query_to_df(
                """
                SELECT *
                FROM futures_report_daily_raw
                WHERE futures_id = ?
                ORDER BY 日期
                """,
                (idx_key,),
            )
        return db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT *
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
              AND 日期 >= ?
              AND 日期 <= ?
            ORDER BY 日期
            """,
            (idx_key, FU._dstr(start_ts), FU._dstr(end_ts)),
        )

    dates_to_fix = pd.to_datetime(target_df["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()

    print(
        f"[backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_volume] futures_id={idx_key}, "
        f"count={len(dates_to_fix)}, "
        f"range=({dates_to_fix[0].strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}~{dates_to_fix[-1].strftime('%Y-%m-%d')})"
    )

    # =========================================================
    # 2. 先補來源資料（避免缺資料）
    # =========================================================
    src_start = dates_to_fix[0]
    src_end = dates_to_fix[-1]

    # 夜盤會用到下一交易日，所以多抓幾天保險
    fm.get_tw_futures_daily(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        start_date=src_start,
        end_date=src_end + pd.Timedelta(days=7),
    )
    fm.get_tw_futures_final_settlement_price(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        start_date=src_start,
        end_date=src_end + pd.Timedelta(days=7),
    )

    # =========================================================
    # 3. 回填 volume
    # =========================================================
    update_rows = []

    for d in dates_to_fix:
        day_row = FU._get_month_contract_row(
            d,
            "position",
            FU._get_front_contract_month(d),
            futures_id,
        )

        if day_row is None:
            print(f"[skip] {idx_key} {FU._dstr(d)} day_row is None")
            continue

        next_d = FU._get_next_after_market_date(d, futures_id)

        night_row = None
        if next_d is not None:
            night_row = FU._get_month_contract_row(
                next_d,
                "after_market",
                FU._get_front_contract_month(next_d),
                futures_id,
            )

        day_vol = (
            float(day_row["volume"])
            if pd.notna(day_row["volume"])
            else None
        )

        night_vol = (
            float(night_row["volume"])
            if night_row is not None and pd.notna(night_row["volume"])
            else None
        )

        update_rows.append(
            (
                day_vol,
                night_vol,
                idx_key,
                FU._dstr(d),
            )
        )

    if not update_rows:
        print("[backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_volume] 沒有可更新列")
    else:
        db.execute_sql(
            """
            UPDATE futures_report_daily_raw
            SET
                日成交量 = ?,
                夜成交量 = ?
            WHERE futures_id = ?
              AND 日期 = ?
            """,
            update_rows,
        )
        print(f"[backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_volume] updated={len(update_rows)}")

    # =========================================================
    # 4. 回傳結果
    # =========================================================
    if start_ts is None or end_ts is None:
        return db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT *
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
            ORDER BY 日期
            """,
            (idx_key,),
        )

    return db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT *
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND 日期 >= ?
          AND 日期 <= ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (idx_key, FU._dstr(start_ts), FU._dstr(end_ts)),
    )

def backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_bollinger(
    start_date=None,
    end_date=None,
    futures_id="TX",
    only_null=True,
    window=20,
    std_mult=2.0,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    回填 futures_report_daily_raw 的布林通道欄位：
    - 日布林標準差
    - 日布林上軌
    - 日布林下軌

    規則：
    - 以 日盤收 為基礎
    - 中軌 = window 日均線（這裡不寫回表，直接用 rolling mean）
    - 標準差 = window 日 rolling std
    - 上軌 = 中軌 + std_mult * 標準差
    - 下軌 = 中軌 - std_mult * 標準差

    參數：
    - start_date, end_date:
        可指定只補某段資料；若都不給，則補整張表
    - futures_id:
        預設 TX
    - only_null:
        True  -> 只補任一布林欄位為 NULL 的列
        False -> 指定區間全部重算覆蓋
    - window:
        布林通道視窗，預設 20
    - std_mult:
        標準差倍數，預設 2.0

    回傳：
    - 補完後的該 futures_id / 區間資料
    """

    idx_key = str(futures_id)

    if window <= 1:
        raise ValueError("window 必須大於 1")

    # =========================================================
    # 1. 找出目標日期
    # =========================================================
    where_sql = ["futures_id = ?"]
    params = [idx_key]

    if start_date is not None:
        start_ts = FU._to_ts(start_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 >= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(start_ts))
    else:
        start_ts = None

    if end_date is not None:
        end_ts = FU._to_ts(end_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 <= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(end_ts))
    else:
        end_ts = None

    if only_null:
        where_sql.append(
            "("
            "日布林標準差 IS NULL OR "
            "日布林上軌 IS NULL OR "
            "日布林下軌 IS NULL"
            ")"
        )

    target_df = db.query_to_df(
        f"""
        SELECT 日期
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE {' AND '.join(where_sql)}
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        tuple(params),
    )

    if target_df is None or target_df.empty:
        print("[backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_bollinger] 無需回填資料")
        if start_ts is None and end_ts is None:
            return db.query_to_df(
                """
                SELECT *
                FROM futures_report_daily_raw
                WHERE futures_id = ?
                ORDER BY 日期
                """,
                (idx_key,),
            )
        else:
            q_where = ["futures_id = ?"]
            q_params = [idx_key]
            if start_ts is not None:
                q_where.append("日期 >= ?")
                q_params.append(FU._dstr(start_ts))
            if end_ts is not None:
                q_where.append("日期 <= ?")
                q_params.append(FU._dstr(end_ts))

            return db.query_to_df(
                f"""
                SELECT *
                FROM futures_report_daily_raw
                WHERE {' AND '.join(q_where)}
                ORDER BY 日期
                """,
                tuple(q_params),
            )

    dates_to_fix = pd.to_datetime(target_df["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()
    min_fix_date = min(dates_to_fix)
    max_fix_date = max(dates_to_fix)

    print(
        f"[backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_bollinger] futures_id={idx_key}, "
        f"count={len(dates_to_fix)}, "
        f"range=({FU._dstr(min_fix_date)}~{FU._dstr(max_fix_date)}), "
        f"only_null={only_null}, window={window}, std_mult={std_mult}"
    )

    # =========================================================
    # 2. 為了計算 rolling，往前多抓 window-1 天
    # =========================================================
    hist_start_df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT 日期
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND 日期 < ?
        ORDER BY 日期 DESC
        LIMIT ?
        """,
        (idx_key, FU._dstr(min_fix_date), window - 1),
    )

    history_dates = []
    if hist_start_df is not None and not hist_start_df.empty:
        history_dates = pd.to_datetime(hist_start_df["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()

    calc_start = min(history_dates) if history_dates else min_fix_date
    calc_end = max_fix_date

    # =========================================================
    # 3. 讀取計算區間資料
    # =========================================================
    calc_df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT 日期, 日盤收
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND 日期 >= ?
          AND 日期 <= ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (idx_key, FU._dstr(calc_start), FU._dstr(calc_end)),
    )

    if calc_df is None or calc_df.empty:
        print("[backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_bollinger] 查無可計算資料")
        return db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT *
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
            ORDER BY 日期
            """,
            (idx_key,),
        )

    calc_df["日期"] = pd.to_datetime(calc_df["日期"])
    calc_df = calc_df.sort_values("日期").copy()

    # =========================================================
    # 4. 計算布林通道
    # =========================================================
    calc_df["bb_ma"] = calc_df["日盤收"].rolling(window=window, min_periods=window).mean()
    calc_df["bb_std"] = calc_df["日盤收"].rolling(window=window, min_periods=window).std()
    calc_df["bb_upper"] = calc_df["bb_ma"] + std_mult * calc_df["bb_std"]
    calc_df["bb_lower"] = calc_df["bb_ma"] - std_mult * calc_df["bb_std"]

    fix_date_set = {pd.Timestamp(x).normalize() for x in dates_to_fix}
    update_rows = []

    for _, row in calc_df.iterrows():
        d = pd.Timestamp(row["日期"]).normalize()
        if d not in fix_date_set:
            continue

        update_rows.append(
            (
                float(row["bb_std"]) if pd.notna(row["bb_std"]) else None,
                float(row["bb_upper"]) if pd.notna(row["bb_upper"]) else None,
                float(row["bb_lower"]) if pd.notna(row["bb_lower"]) else None,
                idx_key,
                d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
            )
        )

    # =========================================================
    # 5. 寫回 DB
    # =========================================================
    if not update_rows:
        print("[backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_bollinger] 沒有可更新列")
    else:
        db.execute_sql(
            """
            UPDATE futures_report_daily_raw
            SET
                日布林標準差 = ?,
                日布林上軌 = ?,
                日布林下軌 = ?
            WHERE futures_id = ?
              AND 日期 = ?
            """,
            update_rows,
        )
        print(f"[backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_bollinger] updated={len(update_rows)}")

    # =========================================================
    # 6. 回傳結果
    # =========================================================
    result_where = ["futures_id = ?"]
    result_params = [idx_key]

    if start_ts is not None:
        result_where.append("日期 >= ?")
        result_params.append(FU._dstr(start_ts))

    if end_ts is not None:
        result_where.append("日期 <= ?")
        result_params.append(FU._dstr(end_ts))

    return db.query_to_df(
        f"""
        SELECT *
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE {' AND '.join(result_where)}
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        tuple(result_params),
    )

def backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_pv(
    start_date=None,
    end_date=None,
    futures_id="TX",
    only_null=True,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    回填 futures_report_daily_raw 的：
    - 日當日價量
    - 日5日價量
    - 日10日價量
    - 日20日價量
    - 日60日價量

    規則：
    - 使用 FU._recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_pv 的邏輯
    - 會自動往前抓足夠資料（60日 window）

    參數：
    - only_null:
        True  -> 只補 NULL
        False -> 全覆蓋

    """

    idx_key = str(futures_id)

    # =========================================================
    # 1. 找目標列
    # =========================================================
    where_sql = ["futures_id = ?"]
    params = [idx_key]

    if start_date is not None:
        start_ts = FU._to_ts(start_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 >= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(start_ts))
    else:
        start_ts = None

    if end_date is not None:
        end_ts = FU._to_ts(end_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 <= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(end_ts))
    else:
        end_ts = None

    if only_null:
        where_sql.append("""
        (
            日當日價量 IS NULL OR
            日5日價量 IS NULL OR
            日10日價量 IS NULL OR
            日20日價量 IS NULL OR
            日60日價量 IS NULL
        )
        """)

    target_df = db.query_to_df(
        f"""
        SELECT 日期
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE {' AND '.join(where_sql)}
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        tuple(params),
    )

    if target_df is None or target_df.empty:
        print("[backfill_pv] 無需回填")
        return db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT *
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
            ORDER BY 日期
            """,
            (idx_key,),
        )

    dates_to_fix = pd.to_datetime(target_df["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()

    print(
        f"[backfill_pv] futures_id={idx_key}, "
        f"count={len(dates_to_fix)}, "
        f"range=({dates_to_fix[0].strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}~{dates_to_fix[-1].strftime('%Y-%m-%d')})"
    )

    # =========================================================
    # 2. 往前補資料（為了 rolling）
    # =========================================================
    hist_df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT 日期
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND 日期 < ?
        ORDER BY 日期 DESC
        LIMIT 60
        """,
        (idx_key, FU._dstr(dates_to_fix[0])),
    )

    history_dates = []
    if hist_df is not None and not hist_df.empty:
        history_dates = pd.to_datetime(hist_df["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()

    calc_start = min(history_dates) if history_dates else dates_to_fix[0]
    calc_end = dates_to_fix[-1]

    # =========================================================
    # 3. 抓資料（整段）
    # =========================================================
    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT
            id,
            日期,
            日盤收,
            日成交量
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND 日期 >= ?
          AND 日期 <= ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (idx_key, FU._dstr(calc_start), FU._dstr(calc_end)),
    )

    if df is None or df.empty:
        print("[backfill_pv] 無資料")
        return

    df["日期"] = pd.to_datetime(df["日期"])
    df = df.sort_values("日期").copy()

    # =========================================================
    # 4. 套用你原本計算邏輯
    # =========================================================
    df["日盤收"] = pd.to_numeric(df["日盤收"], errors="coerce")
    df["日成交量"] = pd.to_numeric(df["日成交量"], errors="coerce")

    df["昨收"] = df["日盤收"].shift(1)
    df["昨量"] = df["日成交量"].shift(1)

    df["漲跌幅_pct"] = (df["日盤收"] - df["昨收"]) / df["昨收"].replace(0, pd.NA)
    df["量增率_pct"] = (df["日成交量"] - df["昨量"]) / df["昨量"].replace(0, pd.NA)

    # === 當日 ===
    df["5MA"] = df["日盤收"].rolling(5).mean()
    df["當日乖離"] = (df["日盤收"] - df["5MA"]) / df["5MA"].replace(0, pd.NA)
    df["當日行情"] = df["當日乖離"].apply(FU._trend_regime)

    df["日當日價量"] = [
        FU._pv_div_by_regime(r, p, v)
        for r, p, v in zip(df["當日行情"], df["漲跌幅_pct"], df["量增率_pct"])
    ]

    # === N日 ===
    for n in [5, 10, 20, 60]:
        df[f"{n}日均量"] = df["日成交量"].shift(1).rolling(n).mean()
        df[f"{n}MA"] = df["日盤收"].shift(1).rolling(n).mean()

        df[f"{n}日乖離"] = (df["日盤收"] - df[f"{n}MA"]) / df[f"{n}MA"].replace(0, pd.NA)

        df[f"{n}日量增率_pct"] = (
            (df["日成交量"] - df[f"{n}日均量"]) / df[f"{n}日均量"].replace(0, pd.NA)
        )

        df[f"{n}日行情"] = df[f"{n}日乖離"].apply(FU._trend_regime)

        df[f"日{n}日價量"] = [
            FU._pv_div_by_regime(r, p, v)
            for r, p, v in zip(
                df[f"{n}日行情"],
                df[f"{n}日乖離"],
                df[f"{n}日量增率_pct"],
            )
        ]

    # =========================================================
    # 5. 只更新 target 範圍
    # =========================================================
    fix_set = {pd.Timestamp(x).normalize() for x in dates_to_fix}

    update_rows = []
    for _, row in df.iterrows():
        d = pd.Timestamp(row["日期"]).normalize()
        if d not in fix_set:
            continue

        update_rows.append(
            (
                row["日當日價量"],
                row["日5日價量"],
                row["日10日價量"],
                row["日20日價量"],
                row["日60日價量"],
                idx_key,
                d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
            )
        )

    if not update_rows:
        print("[backfill_pv] 無更新")
    else:
        db.execute_sql(
            """
            UPDATE futures_report_daily_raw
            SET
                日當日價量 = ?,
                日5日價量 = ?,
                日10日價量 = ?,
                日20日價量 = ?,
                日60日價量 = ?
            WHERE futures_id = ?
              AND 日期 = ?
            """,
            update_rows,
        )
        print(f"[backfill_pv] updated={len(update_rows)}")

    return db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT *
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (idx_key,),
    )

def backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_market_prices(
    start_date=None,
    end_date=None,
    futures_id="TX",
    only_null=True,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    回填 futures_report_daily_raw 的：
    - 加權收盤價
    - 大台收盤價
    - 大台結算價
    """

    idx_key = str(futures_id)

    where_sql = ["futures_id = ?"]
    params = [idx_key]

    if start_date is not None:
        start_ts = FU._to_ts(start_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 >= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(start_ts))
    else:
        start_ts = None

    if end_date is not None:
        end_ts = FU._to_ts(end_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 <= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(end_ts))
    else:
        end_ts = None

    if only_null:
        where_sql.append("""
        (
            加權收盤價 IS NULL OR
            大台收盤價 IS NULL OR
            大台結算價 IS NULL
        )
        """)

    target_df = db.query_to_df(
        f"""
        SELECT 日期
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE {' AND '.join(where_sql)}
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        tuple(params),
    )

    if target_df is None or target_df.empty:
        print("[backfill_market_prices] 無需回填")
        return db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT *
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
            ORDER BY 日期
            """,
            (idx_key,),
        )

    dates_to_fix = pd.to_datetime(target_df["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()

    min_d = min(dates_to_fix)
    max_d = max(dates_to_fix)

    print(
        f"[backfill_market_prices] futures_id={idx_key}, "
        f"count={len(dates_to_fix)}, "
        f"range=({FU._dstr(min_d)}~{FU._dstr(max_d)}), "
        f"only_null={only_null}"
    )

    weighted_close_map, tx_close_map, tx_settlement_map = FU._build_market_price_maps(
        start_ts=min_d,
        end_ts=max_d,
        futures_id=futures_id,
    )

    update_rows = []

    for d in dates_to_fix:
        d_str = FU._dstr(d)

        update_rows.append(
            (
                weighted_close_map.get(d_str),
                tx_close_map.get(d_str),
                tx_settlement_map.get(d_str),
                idx_key,
                d_str,
            )
        )

    if update_rows:
        db.execute_sql(
            """
            UPDATE futures_report_daily_raw
            SET
                加權收盤價 = ?,
                大台收盤價 = ?,
                大台結算價 = ?
            WHERE futures_id = ?
              AND 日期 = ?
            """,
            update_rows,
        )

        print(f"[backfill_market_prices] updated={len(update_rows)}")
    else:
        print("[backfill_market_prices] 無更新資料")

    return db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT *
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (idx_key,),
    )

def backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_month_option(
    start_date=None,
    end_date=None,
    futures_id="TX",
    option_id="TXO",
    only_null=True,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    回填 futures_report_daily_raw：
    - call_月選
    - put_月選

    規則：
    - 來源資料：fm_taiwan_option_daily
    - trading_session 固定 position
    - call_月選 使用 call
    - put_月選 使用 put
    - contract_date：
        第三個週三之前：當月 yyyymm
        第三個週三含當天之後：下一個月 yyyymm
    """

    idx_key = str(futures_id)

    where_sql = ["futures_id = ?"]
    params = [idx_key]

    if start_date is not None:
        start_ts = FU._to_ts(start_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 >= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(start_ts))
    else:
        start_ts = None

    if end_date is not None:
        end_ts = FU._to_ts(end_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 <= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(end_ts))
    else:
        end_ts = None

    if only_null:
        where_sql.append(
            """
            (
                call_月選 IS NULL OR
                put_月選 IS NULL
            )
            """
        )

    target_df = db.query_to_df(
        f"""
        SELECT 日期
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE {' AND '.join(where_sql)}
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        tuple(params),
    )

    if target_df is None or target_df.empty:
        print("[backfill_month_option] 無需回填")
        return db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT *
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
            ORDER BY 日期
            """,
            (idx_key,),
        )

    dates_to_fix = pd.to_datetime(target_df["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()

    print(
        f"[backfill_month_option] futures_id={idx_key}, "
        f"count={len(dates_to_fix)}, "
        f"range=({FU._dstr(dates_to_fix[0])}~{FU._dstr(dates_to_fix[-1])}), "
        f"only_null={only_null}"
    )

    # 如果你有這個 FinMind wrapper，就打開這段
    # 沒有的話先註解掉，因為你的 DB 已經有 fm_taiwan_option_daily
    if hasattr(fm, "get_tw_option_daily"):
        fm.get_tw_option_daily(
            option_id=option_id,
            start_date=dates_to_fix[0],
            end_date=dates_to_fix[-1],
        )

    FU._recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_month_option(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        option_id=option_id,
        target_dates=dates_to_fix,
    )

    return db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT *
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (idx_key,),
    )

def backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_weekly_wed_option(
    start_date=None,
    end_date=None,
    futures_id="TX",
    option_id="TXO",
    only_null=True,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    回填 futures_report_daily_raw：
    - call_週三
    - put_週三

    規則：
    - 來源資料：fm_taiwan_option_daily
    - trading_session 固定 position
    - call_週三 使用 call
    - put_週三 使用 put
    - contract_date：
        從當天所有 LIKE '%W%' 的合約中，
        找第一個「週三到期日 > 交易日」的 contract_date
    - 計算基準：
        加權收盤價
        大台結算價
    """

    idx_key = str(futures_id)

    where_sql = ["futures_id = ?"]
    params = [idx_key]

    if start_date is not None:
        start_ts = FU._to_ts(start_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 >= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(start_ts))
    else:
        start_ts = None

    if end_date is not None:
        end_ts = FU._to_ts(end_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 <= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(end_ts))
    else:
        end_ts = None

    if only_null:
        where_sql.append(
            """
            (
                call_週三 IS NULL OR
                put_週三 IS NULL
            )
            """
        )

    target_df = db.query_to_df(
        f"""
        SELECT 日期
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE {' AND '.join(where_sql)}
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        tuple(params),
    )

    if target_df is None or target_df.empty:
        print("[backfill_weekly_wed_option] 無需回填")
        return db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT *
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
            ORDER BY 日期
            """,
            (idx_key,),
        )

    dates_to_fix = pd.to_datetime(target_df["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()

    print(
        f"[backfill_weekly_wed_option] futures_id={idx_key}, "
        f"count={len(dates_to_fix)}, "
        f"range=({FU._dstr(dates_to_fix[0])}~{FU._dstr(dates_to_fix[-1])}), "
        f"only_null={only_null}"
    )

    if hasattr(fm, "get_tw_option_daily"):
        fm.get_tw_option_daily(
            option_id=option_id,
            start_date=dates_to_fix[0],
            end_date=dates_to_fix[-1],
        )

    FU._recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_weekly_wed_option(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        option_id=option_id,
        target_dates=dates_to_fix,
    )

    return db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT *
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (idx_key,),
    )

def backfill_futures_report_daily_raw_weekly_friday_option(
    start_date=None,
    end_date=None,
    futures_id="TX",
    option_id="TXO",
    only_null=True,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    回填 futures_report_daily_raw：
    - call_週五
    - put_週五
    """

    idx_key = str(futures_id)

    where_sql = ["futures_id = ?"]
    params = [idx_key]

    if start_date is not None:
        start_ts = FU._to_ts(start_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 >= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(start_ts))
    else:
        start_ts = None

    if end_date is not None:
        end_ts = FU._to_ts(end_date)
        where_sql.append("日期 <= ?")
        params.append(FU._dstr(end_ts))
    else:
        end_ts = None

    if only_null:
        where_sql.append(
            """
            (
                call_週五 IS NULL OR
                put_週五 IS NULL
            )
            """
        )

    target_df = db.query_to_df(
        f"""
        SELECT 日期
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE {' AND '.join(where_sql)}
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        tuple(params),
    )

    if target_df is None or target_df.empty:
        print("[backfill_weekly_friday_option] 無需回填")
        return db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT *
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
            ORDER BY 日期
            """,
            (idx_key,),
        )

    dates_to_fix = pd.to_datetime(target_df["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()

    print(
        f"[backfill_weekly_friday_option] futures_id={idx_key}, "
        f"count={len(dates_to_fix)}, "
        f"range=({FU._dstr(dates_to_fix[0])}~{FU._dstr(dates_to_fix[-1])}), "
        f"only_null={only_null}"
    )

    if hasattr(fm, "get_tw_option_daily"):
        fm.get_tw_option_daily(
            option_id=option_id,
            start_date=dates_to_fix[0],
            end_date=dates_to_fix[-1],
        )

    FU._recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_weekly_friday_option(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        option_id=option_id,
        target_dates=dates_to_fix,
    )

    return db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT *
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (idx_key,),
    )

    )

# =========================
# main/stock_report_daily.py
# =========================
import pandas as pd
from datetime import datetime
from dateutil.relativedelta import relativedelta

from module import finMind as fm
from module import stock_report_utils as SRU
from common import db  # DB 模組


today = datetime.now()


# ======================================
# 主流程
# ======================================
def taiex_daily_report(months: int = 4, eDt: datetime = None):
    if eDt is None:
        eDt = datetime.now()
    sDt = eDt - relativedelta(months=months)
    return export("TAIEX", sDt, eDt)


def export(stock_id, sDt, eDt):
    where_condition = """
        AND target_table LIKE 'fm_%' 
        AND target_table LIKE '%_stock_%'
    """
    db._limit_date_span(where_condition=where_condition)
    
    # 建 base df
    df = SRU._build_base_df(fm, stock_id, sDt, eDt)
    if df is None or df.empty:
        print(f"[警告] {stock_id} {sDt} ~ {eDt} 無日資料，export 回傳空 DataFrame")
        return pd.DataFrame()

    # 計算衍生欄位
    df = SRU._compute_derived(df)

    # 寫入 DB
    SRU.upsert(df, stock_id)
    SRU.update_is_complete()

    # 輸出view表
    sql = f"""
        SELECT * FROM v_taiex_report ORDER BY 日期
    """
    output = db.query_to_df(sql)

    output.to_csv("stock_report.csv", index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
    return output


# =========================================================
# 修補指定期間、指定欄位，可選 force_renew
# =========================================================
def repair_stock_report_fields(
    stock_id,
    sDt,
    eDt,
    fields,
    force_renew=False,
    lookback_days=180,
):
    """
    fields: list[str] 要修補的欄位
    force_renew:
      - False: 只補缺值
      - True : 期間內全部重算覆蓋
    """
    sDt_lb = sDt - relativedelta(days=lookback_days)

    base = SRU._build_base_df(fm, stock_id, sDt_lb, eDt)
    if base is None or base.empty:
        print(f"[repair] base empty: {stock_id} {sDt_lb}~{eDt}")
        return 0

    calc = SRU._compute_derived(base)

    calc["日期"] = pd.to_datetime(calc["日期"])
    mask = (
        (calc["日期"] >= pd.to_datetime(sDt.date())) &
        (calc["日期"] <= pd.to_datetime(eDt.date()))
    )
    calc = calc.loc[mask].copy()

    sql_now = f"""
        SELECT 日期, 股票代號, {",".join([f'"{c}"' for c in fields])}
        FROM "{SRU.TABLE}"
        WHERE 股票代號 = '{stock_id}'
          AND 日期 BETWEEN '{sDt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")}' AND '{eDt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")}'
        ORDER BY 日期
    """
    now = db.query_to_df(sql_now)
    if now is None or now.empty:
        print(f"[repair] DB has no rows in range: {stock_id} {sDt}~{eDt}")
        return 0

    now["日期"] = pd.to_datetime(now["日期"])
    calc["日期"] = pd.to_datetime(calc["日期"])

    now2 = now.rename(columns={c: f"{c}_old" for c in fields})
    calc2 = calc.rename(columns={c: f"{c}_new" for c in fields if c in calc.columns})

    merged = now2.merge(
        calc2[["日期"] + [f"{c}_new" for c in fields if f"{c}_new" in calc2.columns]],
        on="日期",
        how="left",
    )
    merged["股票代號"] = stock_id

    patch_rows = []
    for _, r in merged.iterrows():
        out = {"日期": r["日期"], "股票代號": stock_id}
        need_any = False

        for c in fields:
            if c not in SRU.get_field_registry():
                raise ValueError(f"[repair] FIELD_REGISTRY 沒有定義欄位：{c}")

            newv = r.get(f"{c}_new", None)
            oldv = r.get(f"{c}_old", None)

            if SRU._is_missing(newv):
                continue

            if force_renew:
                out[c] = newv
                need_any = True
            else:
                if SRU._is_missing(oldv):
                    out[c] = newv
                    need_any = True

        if need_any:
            patch_rows.append(out)

    if not patch_rows:
        SRU.update_is_complete()
        print("[repair] nothing to patch")
        return 0

    df_patch = pd.DataFrame(patch_rows)

    updated = SRU._update_fields_to_db(
        key_cols=["股票代號", "日期"],
        df_patch=df_patch,
        fields=fields,
    )

    SRU.update_is_complete()
    print(f"[repair] updated rows: {updated}, fields: {fields}, force_renew={force_renew}")
    return updated

# =========================================================
# 🔥 重算百分位 / 級距（專用）
# =========================================================
def rebuild_percentile_fields(
    stock_id,
    sDt,
    eDt,
    force_renew=True,
):
    """
    重算：
    - 漲跌幅百分位數
    - 漲跌幅級距
    - 三大法人買賣超百分位數
    - 三大法人買賣超級距
    - 資金走向判定

    規則：
    - 不往前抓
    - 不夠資料 -> NULL
    - 支援：
        force_renew=False → 只補 NULL
        force_renew=True  → 全覆蓋
    """

    fields = [
        "漲跌幅百分位數",
        "漲跌幅級距",
        "三大法人買賣超百分位數",
        "三大法人買賣超級距",
        "資金走向判定",
    ]

    # ===== build =====
    base = SRU._build_base_df(fm, stock_id, sDt, eDt)
    if base is None or base.empty:
        print(f"[rebuild_pct] base empty: {stock_id}")
        return 0

    calc = SRU._compute_derived(base)

    # ===== 切期間 =====
    calc["日期"] = pd.to_datetime(calc["日期"])

    mask = (
        (calc["日期"] >= pd.to_datetime(sDt.date())) &
        (calc["日期"] <= pd.to_datetime(eDt.date()))
    )
    calc = calc.loc[mask].copy()
    print(calc[[
        "日期",
        "漲跌幅百分位數",
        "漲跌幅級距",
        "三大法人買賣超百分位數",
        "三大法人買賣超級距",
        "資金走向判定",
    ]].tail(30))

    print("calc 非空筆數：")
    for c in [
        "漲跌幅百分位數",
        "漲跌幅級距",
        "三大法人買賣超百分位數",
        "三大法人買賣超級距",
        "資金走向判定",
    ]:
        print(c, calc[c].notna().sum())

    if calc.empty:
        print(f"[rebuild_pct] no calc rows")
        return 0

    # ===== 讀 DB 現有 =====
    sql_now = f"""
        SELECT 日期, 股票代號, {",".join([f'"{c}"' for c in fields])}
        FROM "{SRU.TABLE}"
        WHERE 股票代號 = '{stock_id}'
          AND 日期 BETWEEN '{sDt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")}' AND '{eDt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")}'
    """
    now = db.query_to_df(sql_now)

    if now is None or now.empty:
        print("[rebuild_pct] DB empty")
        return 0

    now["日期"] = pd.to_datetime(now["日期"])

    calc["股票代號"] = stock_id

    merged = now.merge(
        calc[["日期"] + fields],
        on="日期",
        how="left",
        suffixes=("_old", "_new")
    )

    patch_rows = []

    for _, r in merged.iterrows():
        out = {"日期": r["日期"], "股票代號": stock_id}
        need = False

        for c in fields:
            newv = r.get(f"{c}_new")
            oldv = r.get(f"{c}_old")

            if SRU._is_missing(newv):
                continue

            if force_renew:
                out[c] = newv
                need = True
            else:
                if SRU._is_missing(oldv):
                    out[c] = newv
                    need = True

        if need:
            patch_rows.append(out)

    if not patch_rows:
        print("[rebuild_pct] nothing to patch")
        return 0

    df_patch = pd.DataFrame(patch_rows)

    updated = SRU._update_fields_to_db(
        key_cols=["股票代號", "日期"],
        df_patch=df_patch,
        fields=fields,
    )

    SRU.update_is_complete()

    print(f"[rebuild_pct] updated rows: {updated}, force={force_renew}")
    return updated

import requests
import pandas as pd
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from common import tools

twseUrl = "https://www.twse.com.tw/rwd/zh"
common_params = "response=json"

# ======== 注意股公告 ========
# 編號,證券代號,證券名稱,累計,注意交易資訊,公告日期,收盤價,本益比,link
def fetch_notice(type: str, sDt: datetime, eDt: datetime):
    if (sDt > eDt):
        return None

    if eDt > datetime.today():
        eDt = datetime.today()

    match type.lower():
        case "twse":
            apiName = '上市公布注意有價證券資訊'
            apiInfo = tools.get_api_info(apiName)
            apiUrl = apiInfo["url"].iloc[0]
            start_str = sDt.strftime("%Y%m%d")
            end_str = eDt.strftime("%Y%m%d")
            apiParams = {
                "startDate" : start_str,
                "endDate" : end_str,
                "querytype" : "1",
                "stockNo" : None,
                "selectType" : None,
                "sortKind" : "DATE",
                "response" : "json"
            }
            
            # 發送 GET 請求
            response = requests.get(apiUrl, params=apiParams)
            
        case "tpex":
            apiName = '上櫃公布注意有價證券資訊'    
            apiInfo = tools.get_api_info(apiName)
            apiUrl = apiInfo["url"].iloc[0]
            start_str = sDt.strftime("%Y/%m/%d")
            end_str = eDt.strftime("%Y/%m/%d")
            apiParams = {
                "startDate" : start_str,
                "endDate" : end_str,
                "code" : None,
                "cate" : None,
                "type" : "all",
                "order" : "date",
                "id" : None,
                "response" : "json"
            }

            # 發送 POST 請求
            response = requests.post(apiUrl, data=apiParams)
        
        case _:
            return None
        
    response.raise_for_status()  # 檢查 HTTP 錯誤
    
    # 取得回應
    print("Status code:", response.status_code)
    # print("Response text:", response.text)

    # 嘗試把回傳內容轉成 JSON
    raw_data = response.json()

    return raw_data

# ======== 處置股公告 ========
# 編號,公布日期,證券代號,證券名稱,累計,處置起訖時間,處 置原因,處置內容,收盤價,本益比,(memo)
def fetch_punish(type: str, sDt: datetime, eDt: datetime):
    if (sDt > eDt):
        return None

    if eDt > datetime.today():
        eDt = datetime.today()
        
    print(sDt, eDt)

    response = None
    match type.lower():
        case "twse":
            apiName = '上市公布處置有價證券'
            apiInfo = tools.get_api_info(apiName)
            apiUrl = apiInfo["url"].iloc[0]
            
            start_str = sDt.strftime("%Y%m%d")
            end_str = eDt.strftime("%Y%m%d")
            apiParams = {
                "startDate" : start_str,
                "endDate" : end_str,
                "querytype" : 3,
                "stockNo" : None,
                "selectType" : None,
                "proceType" : None,
                "remarkType" : "",
                "sortKind" : "DATE",
                "response" : "json"
            }
            # 發送 GET 請求
            response = requests.get(apiUrl, params=apiParams)
            
        case "tpex":
            apiName = '上櫃處置有價證券資訊'
            apiInfo = tools.get_api_info(apiName)
            apiUrl = apiInfo["url"].iloc[0]
            
            start_str = sDt.strftime("%Y/%m/%d")
            end_str = eDt.strftime("%Y/%m/%d")
            apiParams = {
                "startDate" : start_str,
                "endDate" : end_str,
                "code" : None,
                "cate" : None,
                "type" : "all",
                "reason" : -1,
                "measure" : -1,
                "order" : "date",
                "id" : None,
                "response" : "json"
            }

            # 發送 POST 請求
            response = requests.post(apiUrl, data=apiParams)
            
        case _:
            return None
            
    response.raise_for_status()  # 檢查 HTTP 錯誤
    
    # 取得回應
    print("Status code:", response.status_code)
    # print("Response text:", response.text)

    # 嘗試把回傳內容轉成 JSON
    raw_data = response.json()

    return raw_data

# ======== 4. 融資融券餘額 ========
# 項目,買進,賣出,現金(券)償還,前日餘額,今日餘額
def fetch_margin_trading(date: datetime | None = None):
    date_str = date.strftime("%Y%m%d")
    apiEndpoint = "marginTrading/MI_MARGN"
    apiParams = f"date={date_str}&selectType=MS&{common_params}"
    apiUrl = f"{twseUrl}/{apiEndpoint}?{apiParams}"

    res = requests.get(apiUrl)
    data = res.json()

    # tables[0] 才有資料
    tables = data.get("tables", [])
    if not tables or "fields" not in tables[0] or "data" not in tables[0]:
        print(f"❌ API 回傳格式異常: {data}")
        return None

    return tables[0]

# # ======== 4.2 融資融券餘額 區間版 ========
# # 日期,項目,買進,賣出,現金_券_償還,前日餘額,今日餘額
def fetch_margin_trading_range(sDt: datetime, eDt: datetime):
    if sDt > eDt:
        return None
    
    data = []    
    current = sDt
    while current <= eDt:
        currentData = fetch_margin_trading(current)
        if currentData is None:
            current += timedelta(days=1)
            continue
        
        rows = currentData["data"]
        for aRow in rows:
            aRow.insert(0, current.strftime("%Y%m%d"))
        data = data + rows
        current += timedelta(days=1)
        
    result = {
        "fields": ['日期', '項目', '買進', '賣出', '現金_券_償還', '前日餘額', '今日餘額'],
        "data": data
    }
    return result

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sDt = datetime(2025, 9, 25)
    eDt = datetime(2025, 10, 5)
    data = fetch_punish("twse", sDt, eDt)
    print(data)

import pandas as pd
from datetime import datetime
from dateutil.relativedelta import relativedelta
from common import db
from common import tools
from module import api_getter

# ======== 注意股公告 ========
# 證券代號,證券名稱,累計,注意交易資訊,公告日期,收盤價,本益比,link
def get_notice(type: str, sDt: datetime, eDt: datetime):
    apiName = ""
    match type.lower():
        case "twse":
            apiName = "上市公布注意有價證券資訊"
            
        case "tpex":            
            apiName = "上櫃公布注意有價證券資訊"
        case _:
            return None
        
    return get_time_range_data(apiName, sDt, eDt)

# ======== 處置股公告 ========
# 證券代號,證券名稱,累計,注意交易資訊,公告日期,收盤價,本益比,link
def get_punish(type: str, sDt: datetime, eDt: datetime):
    match type.lower():
        case "twse":
            apiName = "上市公布處置有價證券"
            
        case "tpex":
            apiName = "上櫃處置有價證券資訊"
            
        case _:
            return None
            
    return get_time_range_data(apiName, sDt, eDt)

### 以下為共用function
def get_time_range_data(apiName: str, sDt: datetime, eDt: datetime):
    if (sDt > eDt):
        return None

    if eDt > datetime.today():
        eDt = datetime.today()
    print("[sDt]=" , sDt, "[eDt]=", eDt)

    apiInfo = tools.get_api_info(apiName)
    table = apiInfo["storage_table"].iloc[0]    
    type = apiInfo["type"].iloc[0].lower()
    time_col = apiInfo["time_col"].iloc[0]

    ### 先確認庫資料的上下界
    # 先確認庫有資料
    sql = f"SELECT count(*) FROM {table}"
    dataCnt = int(db.query_single_value(sql))   
    # print([dataCnt])
    
    if (dataCnt < 1):
        maxDt = sDt - relativedelta(days=1)
        minDt = maxDt - relativedelta(days=1)        
    else:        
        sql = f"""
        SELECT
            MIN({time_col}) AS min_date,
            MAX({time_col}) AS max_date
        FROM {table}
        """
        df_check = db.query_to_df(sql)

        minDt = datetime.fromtimestamp(
            tools.roc_to_unix(df_check["min_date"].iloc[0])
        )
        maxDt = datetime.fromtimestamp(
            tools.roc_to_unix(df_check["max_date"].iloc[0])
        )
    print([minDt, maxDt])
    # sys.exit()

    ### 1.資料完全落在庫的範圍，直接搜庫
    if (tools._is_fully_in_range(sDt, eDt, minDt, maxDt)):
        print("*** 直接從庫提取資料")
        sql = f"SELECT * FROM {table}"
        sql += f" WHERE {apiInfo["time_col"].iloc[0]}_ts between {sDt.timestamp()} AND {eDt.timestamp()}"
        df = db.query_to_df(sql)
        return df
    
    ### 2.資料完全落在庫的範圍之外，fetch API
    if (tools._is_no_overlap(sDt, eDt, minDt, maxDt)):
        print("*** 從API提取資料")
        if "注意" in apiName:
            raw_data = api_getter.fetch_notice(type, sDt, eDt)
        elif "處置" in apiName:
            raw_data = api_getter.fetch_punish(type, sDt, eDt)
        else:
            return None    
        
        data = None
        try:
            # 確認 raw_data 是 dict
            if not isinstance(raw_data, dict):
                raise ValueError("API 回傳格式不是 dict")

            match type:
                case "twse":
                    data = raw_data.get("data")
                    if not data or not isinstance(data, list):
                        raise ValueError("API 回傳中沒有 data 或格式錯誤")
                    df = pd.DataFrame(data, columns=raw_data.get("fields"))
                    
                case "tpex":
                    tables = raw_data.get("tables")
                    if not tables or not isinstance(tables, list):
                        raise ValueError("API 回傳中沒有 tables 或格式錯誤")
                    fst_table = tables[0]
                    if not isinstance(fst_table, dict) or "data" not in fst_table:
                        raise ValueError("tables[0] 缺少 data 欄位")
                    data = fst_table["data"]
                    df = pd.DataFrame(raw_data["tables"][0].get("data", []), columns=raw_data["tables"][0].get("fields", []))
                    
                case _:
                    return None
        except Exception as e:
            print("⚠️ API 回傳格式異常:", e)
            print("原始回傳內容:", raw_data)
            return None

        ### 存到db裡
        writeResult = writein_db(apiName, data)
        if not writeResult:
            print("***資料庫寫入失敗！")       
        
        return df
    
    ### 3.重疊的部分取庫，超出的部分用API，最後merge
    ## 找出重疊部分(From 庫)
    overlap_star, overlap_end = tools._overlap_period(sDt, eDt, minDt, maxDt)
    if overlap_star is not None:
        df_exist = get_time_range_data(apiName, overlap_star, overlap_end)

    ## 找出需要獲取的部分
    if (sDt < minDt):
        df_new_left = get_time_range_data(apiName, sDt, minDt - relativedelta(days=1))
    if (maxDt < eDt):
        df_new_right = get_time_range_data(apiName, maxDt + relativedelta(days=1), eDt)
    
    ## 合併 DataFrames
    dfs = []
    if 'df_exist' in locals() and isinstance(df_exist, pd.DataFrame):
        dfs.append(df_exist)
    if 'df_new_left' in locals() and isinstance(df_new_left, pd.DataFrame):
        dfs.append(df_new_left)
    if 'df_new_right' in locals() and isinstance(df_new_right, pd.DataFrame):
        dfs.append(df_new_right)

    if dfs:
        df = pd.concat(dfs, ignore_index=True)
    else:
        df = None  # 如果都不存在，回傳空 DataFrame
    return df
    
def writein_db(apiName, data) -> bool:
    apiInfo = tools.get_api_info(apiName)
    try:
        table = apiInfo["storage_table"].iloc[0]    
    except:
        return False
    
    values = []
    insert_cols = []
    match apiName:
        case "上市公布注意有價證券資訊":
            insert_cols = [
                "證券代號", 
                "證券名稱",
                "累計次數",
                "注意交易資訊", 
                "日期",
                "日期_ts",
                "收盤價",
                "本益比"
            ]
            for r in data:
                try:
                    # 嘗試轉換本益比
                    try:
                        pe_value = float(r[7])
                    except (ValueError, TypeError):
                        pe_value = None
                        
                    values.append((
                        r[1].strip(),
                        r[2].strip(),
                        int(r[3]),
                        r[4].strip(),
                        r[5].strip(),
                        tools.roc_to_unix(r[5].strip()),
                        float(r[6]),
                        pe_value
                    ))
                except:
                    print("Data write in error：", r)
                    continue

        case "上市公布處置有價證券":
            insert_cols = [
                "公布日期",
                "公布日期_ts",
                "證券代號",
                "證券名稱",
                "累計",
                "處置條件",
                "處置起迄時間",
                "處置措施",
                "處置內容",
                "備註"
            ]
            for r in data:
                try:
                    # 跳過當日未處置的資料
                    notice_content = r[7].strip()
                    if notice_content == "":
                        continue
                        
                    # 準備所有資料
                    values.append((
                        r[1].strip(), # 公布日期
                        tools.roc_to_unix(r[1].strip()), # 公布日期_ts
                        r[2].strip(), # 證券代號
                        r[3].strip(), # 證券名稱
                        int(r[4]), # 累計"
                        r[5].strip(), # 處置條件
                        r[6].strip(), # 處置起迄時間
                        r[7].strip(), # 處置措施
                        r[8].strip(), # 處置內容
                        r[9].strip(), # 備註
                    ))
                except:
                    print("Data write in error：", r)
                    continue
            
        case "上櫃公布注意有價證券資訊":
            insert_cols = [
                "證券代號",
                "證券名稱",
                "累計",
                "注意交易資訊",
                "公告日期",
                "公告日期_ts",
                "收盤價",
                "本益比",
                "link"
            ]
            for r in data:
                try:
                    # 跳過當日未處置的資料
                    notice_content = r[7].strip()
                    if notice_content == "":
                        continue
                    
                    # 嘗試轉換本益比
                    try:
                        pe_value = float(r[8])
                    except (ValueError, TypeError):
                        pe_value = None
                    
                    # 準備所有資料
                    values.append((
                        r[1].strip(), # 證券代號
                        r[2].strip(), # 證券名稱
                        int(r[3]), # 累計
                        r[4].strip(), # 注意交易資訊"
                        r[5].strip(), # 公告日期
                        tools.roc_to_unix(r[5].strip()), # 公告日期_ts
                        float(r[6]), # 收盤價
                        pe_value, # 本益比
                        r[8].strip(), # link
                    ))
                except:
                    print("Data write in error：", r)
                    continue
            
        case "上櫃處置有價證券資訊":
            insert_cols = [
                "公布日期", 
                "公布日期_ts",
                "證券代號", 
                "證券名稱", 
                "累計", 
                "處置起訖時間", 
                "處置原因", 
                "處置內容", 
                "收盤價", 
                "本益比", 
                "memo"
            ]
            for r in data:
                try:
                    # 跳過當日未處置的資料
                    punish_content = r[7].strip()
                    if punish_content == "" or punish_content == "本日無處置資料":
                        continue
                    
                    # 嘗試轉換本益比
                    try:
                        pe_value = float(r[9])
                    except (ValueError, TypeError):
                        pe_value = None
                                                
                    # 準備所有資料
                    values.append((
                        r[1].strip(), # 公布日期
                        tools.roc_to_unix(r[1].strip()), # 公布日期_ts
                        r[2].strip(), # 證券代號
                        r[3].strip(), # 證券名稱
                        int(r[4]), # 累計
                        r[5].strip(), # 處置起訖時間
                        r[6].strip(), # 處置原因
                        r[7].strip(), # 處置內容
                        float(r[8]), # 收盤價
                        pe_value, # 本益比
                        r[10].strip() # memo
                    ))
                except:
                    print("Data write in error：", r)
                    continue
        
        case _:
            return False
    
    if len(insert_cols) == 0:
        return False
    
    if len(values) == 0:
        return True
    
    sql = f"""
    INSERT INTO {table}
        ({",".join(insert_cols)})
    VALUES 
        ({", ".join(["?"] * len(insert_cols))})
    ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
    """
    return db.execute_sql(sql, values)

    eDt = datetime(2025, 10, 13)

    print(testData.head())

apiUrl = "https://api.finmindtrade.com/api/v4/data"
api = DataLoader()
token = "eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJ1c2VyX2lkIjoibnljdWxhYjYxNSIsImVtYWlsIjoibnljdWxhYjYxNUBnbWFpbC5jb20iLCJ0b2tlbl92ZXJzaW9uIjoxfQ.-I_DVtVVjIcf5_7EEp53c8bY5fGKQpWDhDM0rtR3sfo"

# 撈取台股清單
def twStockInfo(includeCateHistory:bool=False) -> pd.DataFrame:
    df = None
    output_file = f"{storageDir_twStockInfo}/stock_info.csv"
    if os.path.exists(output_file):
        df = pd.read_csv(output_file)
        print(f"☑️ Data exist: {output_file}")
    else:        
        df = api.taiwan_stock_info() # 台股總覽
        df['date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['date'], errors='coerce') 
        df = df[df['date'].notna()] 
        if not includeCateHistory:
            # 確保 date 欄位是 datetime 格式
            df['date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['date'])

            # 依 stock_id 分組，選取每組中 date 最大的那筆資料
            latest_df = df.sort_values('date').groupby('stock_id', as_index=False).tail(1)

            # 依照 stock_id 排序（可選）
            df = latest_df.sort_values(by='stock_id')
        df.to_csv(output_file, index=False, encoding='utf-8-sig')
    return df

# 撈取上市清單
def twStockInfoTwse(includeCateHistory:bool=False) -> pd.DataFrame:
    df_twse_filtered = None
    output_file = f"{storageDir_twStockInfo}/stock_info-twse.csv"
    if os.path.exists(output_file):
        df = pd.read_csv(output_file)
        print(f"☑️ Data exist: {output_file}")
        return df
    else:        
        df = twStockInfo(includeCateHistory)
        
        # 篩選 type 為 'twse' (上市)
        df_twse = df[df['type'] == 'twse']

        # 排除 industry_category 欄位含有指定關鍵字的資料
        exclude_keywords = ['ETF', 'Index', '受益證券', 'ETN', '大盤', '存託憑證', '創新板股票', '創新版股票']
        pattern = '|'.join(exclude_keywords)  # 建立 regex 模式
        df_twse_filtered = df_twse[~df_twse['industry_category'].str.contains(pattern, na=False)]

        df_twse_filtered.to_csv(output_file, index=False, encoding='utf-8-sig')
    return df_twse_filtered

# 排除興櫃的台股清單
def twStockInfoNoEmerging(includeCateHistory:bool=False) -> pd.DataFrame:
    df_twse_filtered = None
    output_file = f"{storageDir_twStockInfo}/stock_info-no_emerging.csv"
    if os.path.exists(output_file):
        df_twse_filtered = pd.read_csv(output_file)
        print(f"☑️ Data exist: {output_file}")
    else:          
        df = twStockInfo(includeCateHistory)
        
        # 排除 type 為 'emerging' (興櫃)
        df_twse = df[df['type'] != 'emerging']

        # 排除 industry_category 欄位含有指定關鍵字的資料
        exclude_keywords = ['ETF', 'Index', '受益證券', 'ETN', '大盤', '存託憑證', '創新板股票', '創新版股票']
        pattern = '|'.join(exclude_keywords)  # 建立 regex 模式
        df_twse_filtered = df_twse[~df_twse['industry_category'].str.contains(pattern, na=False)]

        df_twse_filtered.to_csv(output_file, index=False, encoding='utf-8-sig')
    return df_twse_filtered
