from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd
from common import db
from module import finMind as fm

# =========================================================
# 共用工具
# =========================================================
def _to_ts(x) -> pd.Timestamp:
    return pd.Timestamp(x).normalize()


def _dstr(x) -> str:
    return _to_ts(x).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")


def _weekday_text(ts: pd.Timestamp) -> str:
    mapping = {
        0: "星期一",
        1: "星期二",
        2: "星期三",
        3: "星期四",
        4: "星期五",
        5: "星期六",
        6: "星期日",
    }
    return mapping[ts.weekday()]


def _day_type_text(ts: pd.Timestamp) -> str:
    return "週間" if ts.weekday() < 5 else "假日"


def _week_no(ts: pd.Timestamp) -> int:
    return int(ts.isocalendar().week)


def _sync_date_span_from_table(
    target_table: str,
    idx_key: str,
    idx_field: str,
    date_field: str,
):
    span_df = db.query_to_df(
        f"""
        SELECT MIN({date_field}) AS min_date, MAX({date_field}) AS max_date
        FROM {target_table}
        WHERE {idx_field} = ?
        """,
        (str(idx_key),),
    )

    actual_s = (
        pd.Timestamp(span_df.loc[0, "min_date"]).normalize()
        if not span_df.empty and pd.notna(span_df.loc[0, "min_date"])
        else None
    )
    actual_e = (
        pd.Timestamp(span_df.loc[0, "max_date"]).normalize()
        if not span_df.empty and pd.notna(span_df.loc[0, "max_date"])
        else None
    )

    print(
        f"[sync_date_span] table={target_table}, idx_key={idx_key}, "
        f"actual_s={_dstr(actual_s) if actual_s is not None else None}, "
        f"actual_e={_dstr(actual_e) if actual_e is not None else None}"
    )

    if actual_s is None or actual_e is None:
        return

    db.execute_sql(
        """
        INSERT INTO date_span (target_table, idx_key, start_date, end_date, updated_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
        ON CONFLICT(target_table, idx_key) DO UPDATE SET
          start_date = excluded.start_date,
          end_date   = excluded.end_date,
          updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        """,
        (target_table, str(idx_key), _dstr(actual_s), _dstr(actual_e)),
    )

# =========================================================
# 來源資料 helper
# =========================================================
def _get_trading_dates(start_date, end_date, futures_id="TX") -> list[pd.Timestamp]:
    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT DISTINCT date
        FROM fm_taiwan_futures_daily
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND trading_session = 'position'
          AND date >= ?
          AND date <= ?
        ORDER BY date
        """,
        (futures_id, _dstr(start_date), _dstr(end_date)),
    )
    if df.empty:
        return []
    return [pd.Timestamp(x).normalize() for x in df["date"].tolist()]


def _get_recent_trading_dates(limit=5, futures_id="TX") -> list[pd.Timestamp]:
    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT DISTINCT date
        FROM fm_taiwan_futures_daily
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND trading_session = 'position'
        ORDER BY date DESC
        LIMIT ?
        """,
        (futures_id, int(limit)),
    )
    if df.empty:
        return []
    return sorted(pd.Timestamp(x).normalize() for x in df["date"].tolist())


def _get_next_trading_date(trading_date, futures_id="TX") -> pd.Timestamp | None:
    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT MIN(date) AS next_date
        FROM fm_taiwan_futures_daily
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND trading_session = 'position'
          AND date > ?
        """,
        (futures_id, _dstr(trading_date)),
    )
    if df.empty or pd.isna(df.loc[0, "next_date"]):
        return None
    return pd.Timestamp(df.loc[0, "next_date"]).normalize()

def _get_next_after_market_date(trading_date, futures_id="TX") -> pd.Timestamp | None:
    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT MIN(date) AS next_date
        FROM fm_taiwan_futures_daily
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND trading_session = 'after_market'
          AND date > ?
        """,
        (futures_id, _dstr(trading_date)),
    )
    if df.empty or pd.isna(df.loc[0, "next_date"]):
        return None
    return pd.Timestamp(df.loc[0, "next_date"]).normalize()

def _get_front_contract_month(trading_date, futures_id="TX") -> str | None:
    """
    用實際最後結算日判斷近月：
    找出結算日 >= trading_date 的第一個月合約
    """
    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT date, contract_month
        FROM fm_taiwan_futures_final_settlement_price
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND futures_type = 'index'
          AND contract_month NOT LIKE '%W%'
          AND LENGTH(contract_month) = 6
          AND date >= ?
        ORDER BY date ASC
        LIMIT 1
        """,
        (futures_id, _dstr(trading_date)),
    )
    if df.empty:
        return None
    return str(df.loc[0, "contract_month"])


def _get_month_contract_row(
    trading_date,
    trading_session: str,
    contract_month: str | None,
    futures_id="TX",
) -> pd.Series | None:
    """
    先抓指定 contract_month。
    若抓不到，備援用當天該 session 成交量最大的純月合約。
    """
    if contract_month is not None:
        df = db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT date, futures_id, contract_date, open, max, min, close, volume, trading_session
            FROM fm_taiwan_futures_daily
            WHERE futures_id = ?
              AND date = ?
              AND trading_session = ?
              AND contract_date = ?
            LIMIT 1
            """,
            (futures_id, _dstr(trading_date), trading_session, contract_month),
        )
        if not df.empty:
            return df.iloc[0]

    df_fb = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT date, futures_id, contract_date, open, max, min, close, volume, trading_session
        FROM fm_taiwan_futures_daily
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND date = ?
          AND trading_session = ?
          AND contract_date NOT LIKE '%/%'
          AND contract_date NOT LIKE '%W%'
          AND LENGTH(contract_date) = 6
        ORDER BY volume DESC, contract_date ASC
        LIMIT 1
        """,
        (futures_id, _dstr(trading_date), trading_session),
    )
    if df_fb.empty:
        return None
    return df_fb.iloc[0]



def _get_incomplete_night_dates(
    start_date,
    end_date,
    futures_id="TX",
) -> list[pd.Timestamp]:
    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT 日期
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND 日期 >= ?
          AND 日期 <= ?
          AND (
                夜盤開 IS NULL
             OR 夜盤收 IS NULL
             OR 夜盤高 IS NULL
             OR 夜盤低 IS NULL
          )
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (futures_id, _dstr(start_date), _dstr(end_date)),
    )
    if df.empty:
        return []
    return [pd.Timestamp(x).normalize() for x in df["日期"].tolist()]


# =========================================================
# MA 重算
# =========================================================
def _recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_ma(futures_id="TX"):
    """
    只重算指定 futures_id 的 futures_report_daily_raw MA 欄位
    """
    fid = str(futures_id)

    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT
            id,
            日期,
            日盤收,
            夜盤收
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (fid,),
    )

    if df.empty:
        return

    df = df.copy()
    df["日盤收"] = pd.to_numeric(df["日盤收"], errors="coerce")
    df["夜盤收"] = pd.to_numeric(df["夜盤收"], errors="coerce")

    for win in [5, 10, 20, 60, 120]:
        df[f"日{win}MA"] = df["日盤收"].rolling(win, min_periods=win).mean()
        df[f"夜{win}MA"] = df["夜盤收"].rolling(win, min_periods=win).mean()

    params = list(
        df[
            [
                "日5MA", "日10MA", "日20MA", "日60MA", "日120MA",
                "夜5MA", "夜10MA", "夜20MA", "夜60MA", "夜120MA",
                "id",
            ]
        ].itertuples(index=False, name=None)
    )

    if not params:
        return

    sql = """
    UPDATE futures_report_daily_raw
    SET
        日5MA   = ?,
        日10MA  = ?,
        日20MA  = ?,
        日60MA  = ?,
        日120MA = ?,
        夜5MA   = ?,
        夜10MA  = ?,
        夜20MA  = ?,
        夜60MA  = ?,
        夜120MA = ?
    WHERE id = ?
    """
    db.execute_sql(sql, params)



def _trend_regime(price_vs_ma):
    if pd.isna(price_vs_ma):
        return None
    if price_vs_ma > 0:
        return "多頭行情"
    if price_vs_ma < 0:
        return "空頭行情"
    return "中性"


def _pv_div_by_regime(regime, price_change, vol_change):
    if pd.isna(price_change) or pd.isna(vol_change) or regime is None:
        return None

    p_up = price_change > 0
    p_dn = price_change < 0
    v_up = vol_change > 0
    v_dn = vol_change < 0

    final_str = ""

    if p_up:
        final_str += "價漲"
    elif p_dn:
        final_str += "價跌"
    else:
        final_str += "價同"

    if v_up:
        final_str += "量增"
    elif v_dn:
        final_str += "量減"
    else:
        final_str += "量同"

    return final_str

def _recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_pv(futures_id="TX"):
    """
    回填：
    日當日價量 / 日5日價量 / 日10日價量 / 日20日價量 / 日60日價量
    """

    fid = str(futures_id)

    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT
            id,
            日期,
            日盤收,
            日成交量
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = ?
        ORDER BY 日期
        """,
        (fid,),
    )

    if df.empty:
        return

    df = df.copy()

    # === 基本欄位 ===
    df["日盤收"] = pd.to_numeric(df["日盤收"], errors="coerce")
    df["日成交量"] = pd.to_numeric(df["日成交量"], errors="coerce")

    # === 昨收 / 昨量 ===
    df["昨收"] = df["日盤收"].shift(1)
    df["昨量"] = df["日成交量"].shift(1)

    df["漲跌幅_pct"] = (df["日盤收"] - df["昨收"]) / df["昨收"].replace(0, pd.NA)
    df["量增率_pct"] = (df["日成交量"] - df["昨量"]) / df["昨量"].replace(0, pd.NA)

    # =========================
    # 當日價量（用5MA判斷行情）
    # =========================
    df["5MA"] = df["日盤收"].shift(1).rolling(5).mean()
    df["當日乖離"] = (df["日盤收"] - df["5MA"]) / df["5MA"].replace(0, pd.NA)

    df["當日行情"] = df["當日乖離"].apply(_trend_regime)

    df["日當日價量"] = [
        _pv_div_by_regime(regime, pchg, vchg)
        for regime, pchg, vchg in zip(
            df["當日行情"],
            df["漲跌幅_pct"],
            df["量增率_pct"],
        )
    ]

    # =========================
    # N日價量
    # =========================
    for n in [5, 10, 20, 60]:
        df[f"{n}日均量"] = df["日成交量"].shift(1).rolling(n).mean()
        df[f"{n}MA"] = df["日盤收"].shift(1).rolling(n).mean()

        df[f"{n}日乖離"] = (df["日盤收"] - df[f"{n}MA"]) / df[f"{n}MA"].replace(0, pd.NA)

        df[f"{n}日量增率_pct"] = (
            (df["日成交量"] - df[f"{n}日均量"]) / df[f"{n}日均量"].replace(0, pd.NA)
        )

        df[f"{n}日行情"] = df[f"{n}日乖離"].apply(_trend_regime)

        df[f"日{n}日價量"] = [
            _pv_div_by_regime(regime, pchg, vchg)
            for regime, pchg, vchg in zip(
                df[f"{n}日行情"],
                df[f"{n}日乖離"],
                df[f"{n}日量增率_pct"],
            )
        ]

    # =========================
    # 寫回 DB
    # =========================
    update_rows = list(
        df[
            [
                "日當日價量",
                "日5日價量",
                "日10日價量",
                "日20日價量",
                "日60日價量",
                "id",
            ]
        ].itertuples(index=False, name=None)
    )

    if not update_rows:
        return

    db.execute_sql(
        """
        UPDATE futures_report_daily_raw
        SET
            日當日價量 = ?,
            日5日價量 = ?,
            日10日價量 = ?,
            日20日價量 = ?,
            日60日價量 = ?
        WHERE id = ?
        """,
        update_rows,
    )

def _safe_float(x):
    if pd.isna(x):
        return None
    return float(x)


def _build_market_price_maps(
    start_ts,
    end_ts,
    futures_id="TX",
):
    """
    建立三個日期對照表：
    - 加權收盤價：TAIEX close
    - 大台收盤價：TX position 近月 close
    - 大台結算價：TX position 近月 settlement_price

    規則：
    - 原則上取 contract_date 最小的月合約
    - 但若該筆 settlement_price = 0，代表可能是結算日已結算合約
    - 則改取下一個 contract_date 較大的月合約
    """

    # =====================================================
    # 1. 加權收盤價：TAIEX close
    # =====================================================
    df_taiex = fm.get_tw_stock_daily_price(
        stock_id="TAIEX",
        start_date=start_ts,
        end_date=end_ts,
    )

    weighted_close_map = {}

    if df_taiex is not None and not df_taiex.empty:
        df_taiex = df_taiex.copy()
        df_taiex["date"] = pd.to_datetime(df_taiex["date"]).dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

        weighted_close_map = {
            row["date"]: _safe_float(row["close"])
            for _, row in df_taiex.iterrows()
        }

    # =====================================================
    # 2. 大台收盤價 / 大台結算價
    # =====================================================
    df_tx = fm.get_tw_futures_daily(
        futures_id=futures_id,
        start_date=start_ts,
        end_date=end_ts,
    )

    tx_close_map = {}
    tx_settlement_map = {}

    if df_tx is not None and not df_tx.empty:
        df_tx = df_tx.copy()
        df_tx["date"] = pd.to_datetime(df_tx["date"]).dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        df_tx["contract_date"] = df_tx["contract_date"].astype(str)
        df_tx["trading_session"] = df_tx["trading_session"].astype(str)

        df_tx["close"] = pd.to_numeric(df_tx["close"], errors="coerce")
        df_tx["settlement_price"] = pd.to_numeric(df_tx["settlement_price"], errors="coerce")

        df_tx = df_tx[
            (df_tx["trading_session"] == "position")
            & (df_tx["contract_date"].str.len() == 6)
        ].copy()

        if not df_tx.empty:
            df_tx = df_tx.sort_values(["date", "contract_date"]).copy()

            selected_rows = []

            for date_str, g in df_tx.groupby("date", sort=True):
                g = g.sort_values("contract_date").copy()

                valid_g = g[
                    g["settlement_price"].notna()
                    & (g["settlement_price"] != 0)
                ]

                if not valid_g.empty:
                    selected_rows.append(valid_g.iloc[0])
                else:
                    selected_rows.append(g.iloc[0])

            df_front = pd.DataFrame(selected_rows)

            tx_close_map = {
                row["date"]: _safe_float(row["close"])
                for _, row in df_front.iterrows()
            }

            tx_settlement_map = {
                row["date"]: _safe_float(row["settlement_price"])
                for _, row in df_front.iterrows()
            }

    return weighted_close_map, tx_close_map, tx_settlement_map

# =========================================================
# 月選 Call / Put total 計算
# =========================================================
def _third_wednesday(year: int, month: int) -> pd.Timestamp:
    """
    回傳該年月的第三個星期三
    """
    first_day = pd.Timestamp(year=year, month=month, day=1)

    # weekday: Monday=0, Wednesday=2
    days_to_wed = (2 - first_day.weekday()) % 7
    first_wed = first_day + pd.Timedelta(days=days_to_wed)

    third_wed = first_wed + pd.Timedelta(days=14)
    return third_wed.normalize()


def _yyyymm_add_month(ts: pd.Timestamp, months: int = 1) -> str:
    """
    yyyy-mm-dd + N 個月後，回傳 yyyymm
    """
    ts = pd.Timestamp(ts).normalize()
    y = ts.year
    m = ts.month + months

    while m > 12:
        y += 1
        m -= 12

    while m < 1:
        y -= 1
        m += 12

    return f"{y}{m:02d}"


def _get_month_option_contract_month(trading_date) -> str:
    """
    月選 contract_date 規則：
    - 第三個週三之前：當月 yyyymm
    - 第三個週三含當天之後：下一個月 yyyymm
    """
    d = pd.Timestamp(trading_date).normalize()
    third_wed = _third_wednesday(d.year, d.month)

    if d >= third_wed:
        return _yyyymm_add_month(d, 1)

    return f"{d.year}{d.month:02d}"


def _calc_month_option_row_value(
    call_put: str,
    strike_price,
    settlement_price,
    open_interest,
    weighted_close,
    tx_close,
):
    """
    單一履約價的月選價值。

    依照你的 Google Sheet 公式：

    Call:
        if 履約價 < 加權收盤價:
            結算價 - (大台收盤價 - 履約價)
        else:
            結算價

    Put:
        if 履約價 > 加權收盤價:
            結算價 + (大台收盤價 - 履約價)
        else:
            結算價
    """

    if pd.isna(strike_price) or pd.isna(settlement_price):
        return None

    if pd.isna(weighted_close) or pd.isna(tx_close):
        return None

    if pd.isna(open_interest):
        open_interest = 0

    cp = str(call_put).strip().lower()

    strike_price = float(strike_price)
    settlement_price = float(settlement_price)
    open_interest = float(open_interest)
    weighted_close = float(weighted_close)
    tx_close = float(tx_close)

    if cp == "call":
        if strike_price < weighted_close:
            value = settlement_price - (tx_close - strike_price)
        else:
            value = settlement_price

    elif cp == "put":
        if strike_price > weighted_close:
            value = settlement_price + (tx_close - strike_price)
        else:
            value = settlement_price
    else:
        return None
    
    if value < 0:
        value = 0
    return value


def _calc_month_option_total_for_day(
    trading_date,
    call_put: str,
    option_id="TXO",
):
    """
    計算單日 call_月選 或 put_月選 total。

    資料來源：
    - fm_taiwan_option_daily
    - futures_report_daily_raw

    必要條件：
    - futures_report_daily_raw 當天要已經有：
        加權收盤價
        大台收盤價
    """

    d = pd.Timestamp(trading_date).normalize()
    d_str = _dstr(d)

    cp = str(call_put).strip().lower()
    if cp not in ("call", "put"):
        raise ValueError("call_put 只能是 call 或 put")

    contract_month = _get_month_option_contract_month(d)

    # =====================================================
    # 1. 讀取當天加權收盤價 / 大台收盤價
    # =====================================================
    price_df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT
            加權收盤價,
            大台收盤價
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = 'TX'
          AND 日期 = ?
        LIMIT 1
        """,
        (d_str,),
    )

    if price_df is None or price_df.empty:
        print(f"[month_option][skip] {d_str} futures_report_daily_raw 無價格資料")
        return None

    weighted_close = price_df.loc[0, "加權收盤價"]
    tx_close = price_df.loc[0, "大台收盤價"]

    if pd.isna(weighted_close) or pd.isna(tx_close):
        print(f"[month_option][skip] {d_str} 加權收盤價 / 大台收盤價 為 NULL")
        return None

    # =====================================================
    # 2. 讀取 TXO 月選資料
    # =====================================================
    opt_df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT
            option_id,
            contract_date,
            strike_price,
            call_put,
            settlement_price,
            open_interest
        FROM fm_taiwan_option_daily
        WHERE date = ?
          AND trading_session = 'position'
          AND LOWER(call_put) = ?
          AND contract_date = ?
        ORDER BY CAST(strike_price AS REAL)
        """,
        (d_str, cp, contract_month),
    )

    if opt_df is None or opt_df.empty:
        print(
            f"[month_option][skip] {d_str} "
            f"cp={cp}, contract_month={contract_month}, 查無 TXO 月選資料"
        )
        return None

    # =====================================================
    # 3. 逐筆計算後加總
    # =====================================================
    total = 0.0
    has_value = False

    for _, row in opt_df.iterrows():
        row_value = _calc_month_option_row_value(
            call_put=row["call_put"],
            strike_price=row["strike_price"],
            settlement_price=row["settlement_price"],
            open_interest=row["open_interest"],
            weighted_close=weighted_close,
            tx_close=tx_close,
        )

        if row_value is None or pd.isna(row_value):
            continue

        total += float(row_value)
        has_value = True

    if not has_value:
        return None

    return total


def _recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_month_option(
    futures_id="TX",
    option_id="TXO",
    target_dates=None,
):
    """
    回填 futures_report_daily_raw：
    - call_月選
    - put_月選

    若 target_dates=None，則重算整張 futures_report_daily_raw。
    """

    fid = str(futures_id)

    if target_dates is None:
        df_dates = db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT 日期
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
            ORDER BY 日期
            """,
            (fid,),
        )

        if df_dates is None or df_dates.empty:
            return

        dates = pd.to_datetime(df_dates["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()

    else:
        dates = [
            pd.Timestamp(x).normalize()
            for x in target_dates
            if x is not None
        ]

    if not dates:
        return

    update_rows = []

    for d in dates:
        d_str = _dstr(d)

        call_total = _calc_month_option_total_for_day(
            trading_date=d,
            call_put="call",
            option_id=option_id,
        )

        put_total = _calc_month_option_total_for_day(
            trading_date=d,
            call_put="put",
            option_id=option_id,
        )

        update_rows.append(
            (
                call_total,
                put_total,
                fid,
                d_str,
            )
        )

    if not update_rows:
        return

    db.execute_sql(
        """
        UPDATE futures_report_daily_raw
        SET
            call_月選 = ?,
            put_月選 = ?
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND 日期 = ?
        """,
        update_rows,
    )

    print(f"[month_option] updated={len(update_rows)}")

# =========================================================
# 週三選 Call / Put total 計算
# =========================================================
def _nth_weekday_of_month(year: int, month: int, weekday: int, nth: int) -> pd.Timestamp:
    """
    回傳指定年月第 nth 個 weekday 的日期。

    weekday:
        Monday=0
        Tuesday=1
        Wednesday=2
        Thursday=3
        Friday=4
    """
    first_day = pd.Timestamp(year=year, month=month, day=1)

    days_to_target = (weekday - first_day.weekday()) % 7
    first_target = first_day + pd.Timedelta(days=days_to_target)

    target_day = first_target + pd.Timedelta(days=7 * (nth - 1))
    return target_day.normalize()


def _parse_weekly_option_contract_date(contract_date: str) -> pd.Timestamp | None:
    """
    解析週選 contract_date。

    e.g.
        202604W5 -> 2026年04月第5個週三
        202605W1 -> 2026年05月第1個週三
    """
    if contract_date is None:
        return None

    s = str(contract_date).strip().upper()

    if "W" not in s:
        return None

    try:
        ym, w_part = s.split("W", 1)

        if len(ym) != 6:
            return None

        year = int(ym[:4])
        month = int(ym[4:6])
        nth = int(w_part)

        return _nth_weekday_of_month(
            year=year,
            month=month,
            weekday=2,  # Wednesday
            nth=nth,
        )

    except Exception:
        return None


def _get_weekly_wed_option_contract_date(
    trading_date,
    call_put: str = "call",
) -> str | None:
    """
    找出交易日當天要使用的週三選 contract_date。

    規則：
    1. 先查當天 fm_taiwan_option_daily 內所有 W 合約
    2. 將 contract_date 轉成實際週三到期日
    3. 找第一個 到期日 > trading_date 的 contract_date
    """
    d = pd.Timestamp(trading_date).normalize()
    d_str = _dstr(d)

    cp = str(call_put).strip().lower()

    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT DISTINCT contract_date
        FROM fm_taiwan_option_daily
        WHERE date = ?
          AND trading_session = 'position'
          AND LOWER(call_put) = ?
          AND contract_date LIKE '%W%'
        ORDER BY contract_date
        """,
        (d_str, cp),
    )

    if df is None or df.empty:
        return None

    candidates = []

    for contract_date in df["contract_date"].tolist():
        expiry_date = _parse_weekly_option_contract_date(contract_date)

        if expiry_date is None:
            continue

        if expiry_date > d:
            candidates.append(
                (
                    expiry_date,
                    str(contract_date),
                )
            )

    if not candidates:
        return None

    candidates = sorted(candidates, key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))
    return candidates[0][1]


def _calc_option_row_value(
    call_put: str,
    strike_price,
    settlement_price,
    weighted_close,
    tx_base_price,
):
    """
    單一選擇權履約價價值。

    Call:
        if 履約價 < 加權收盤價:
            結算價 - (大台基準價 - 履約價)
        else:
            結算價

    Put:
        if 履約價 > 加權收盤價:
            結算價 + (大台基準價 - 履約價)
        else:
            結算價

    規則：
        - 不乘 open_interest
        - 小於 0 的值改算 0
    """

    if pd.isna(strike_price) or pd.isna(settlement_price):
        return None

    if pd.isna(weighted_close) or pd.isna(tx_base_price):
        return None

    cp = str(call_put).strip().lower()

    strike_price = float(strike_price)
    settlement_price = float(settlement_price)
    weighted_close = float(weighted_close)
    tx_base_price = float(tx_base_price)

    if cp == "call":
        if strike_price < weighted_close:
            value = settlement_price - (tx_base_price - strike_price)
        else:
            value = settlement_price

    elif cp == "put":
        if strike_price > weighted_close:
            value = settlement_price + (tx_base_price - strike_price)
        else:
            value = settlement_price

    else:
        return None

    if value < 0:
        value = 0

    return value

def _calc_weekly_wed_option_total_for_day(
    trading_date,
    call_put: str,
    option_id="TXO",
):
    """
    計算單日：
    - call_週三
    - put_週三

    使用：
    - 加權收盤價
    - 大台結算價
    - fm_taiwan_option_daily 的週選 W 合約
    """

    d = pd.Timestamp(trading_date).normalize()
    d_str = _dstr(d)

    cp = str(call_put).strip().lower()
    if cp not in ("call", "put"):
        raise ValueError("call_put 只能是 call 或 put")

    contract_date = _get_weekly_wed_option_contract_date(
        trading_date=d,
        call_put=cp,
    )

    if contract_date is None:
        print(f"[weekly_wed_option][skip] {d_str} cp={cp} 找不到週三選 contract_date")
        return None

    # =====================================================
    # 1. 讀取加權收盤價 / 大台結算價
    # =====================================================
    price_df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT
            加權收盤價,
            大台結算價
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = 'TX'
          AND 日期 = ?
        LIMIT 1
        """,
        (d_str,),
    )

    if price_df is None or price_df.empty:
        print(f"[weekly_wed_option][skip] {d_str} futures_report_daily_raw 無價格資料")
        return None

    weighted_close = price_df.loc[0, "加權收盤價"]
    tx_settlement = price_df.loc[0, "大台結算價"]

    if pd.isna(weighted_close) or pd.isna(tx_settlement):
        print(f"[weekly_wed_option][skip] {d_str} 加權收盤價 / 大台結算價 為 NULL")
        return None

    # =====================================================
    # 2. 讀取週三選資料
    # =====================================================
    opt_df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT
            option_id,
            contract_date,
            strike_price,
            call_put,
            settlement_price,
            open_interest
        FROM fm_taiwan_option_daily
        WHERE date = ?
          AND trading_session = 'position'
          AND LOWER(call_put) = ?
          AND contract_date = ?
        ORDER BY contract_date, CAST(strike_price AS REAL)
        """,
        (d_str, cp, contract_date),
    )

    if opt_df is None or opt_df.empty:
        print(
            f"[weekly_wed_option][skip] {d_str} "
            f"cp={cp}, contract_date={contract_date}, 查無資料"
        )
        return None

    # =====================================================
    # 3. 逐筆計算，排除負值後加總
    # =====================================================
    total = 0.0
    has_value = False

    for _, row in opt_df.iterrows():
        row_value = _calc_option_row_value(
            call_put=row["call_put"],
            strike_price=row["strike_price"],
            settlement_price=row["settlement_price"],
            weighted_close=weighted_close,
            tx_base_price=tx_settlement,
        )

        if row_value is None or pd.isna(row_value):
            continue

        total += float(row_value)
        has_value = True

    if not has_value:
        return None

    return total


def _recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_weekly_wed_option(
    futures_id="TX",
    option_id="TXO",
    target_dates=None,
):
    """
    回填 futures_report_daily_raw：
    - call_週三
    - put_週三

    若 target_dates=None，則重算整張 futures_report_daily_raw。
    """

    fid = str(futures_id)

    if target_dates is None:
        df_dates = db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT 日期
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
            ORDER BY 日期
            """,
            (fid,),
        )

        if df_dates is None or df_dates.empty:
            return

        dates = pd.to_datetime(df_dates["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()

    else:
        dates = [
            pd.Timestamp(x).normalize()
            for x in target_dates
            if x is not None
        ]

    if not dates:
        return

    update_rows = []

    for d in dates:
        d_str = _dstr(d)

        call_total = _calc_weekly_wed_option_total_for_day(
            trading_date=d,
            call_put="call",
            option_id=option_id,
        )

        put_total = _calc_weekly_wed_option_total_for_day(
            trading_date=d,
            call_put="put",
            option_id=option_id,
        )

        update_rows.append(
            (
                call_total,
                put_total,
                fid,
                d_str,
            )
        )

    if not update_rows:
        return

    db.execute_sql(
        """
        UPDATE futures_report_daily_raw
        SET
            call_週三 = ?,
            put_週三 = ?
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND 日期 = ?
        """,
        update_rows,
    )

    print(f"[weekly_wed_option] updated={len(update_rows)}")

# =========================================================
# 週五選 Call / Put total 計算
# =========================================================
def _parse_weekly_friday_option_contract_date(contract_date: str) -> pd.Timestamp | None:
    """
    解析週五選 contract_date。

    e.g.
        202605F1 -> 2026年05月第1個週五
        202605F2 -> 2026年05月第2個週五
    """
    if contract_date is None:
        return None

    s = str(contract_date).strip().upper()

    if "F" not in s:
        return None

    try:
        ym, f_part = s.split("F", 1)

        if len(ym) != 6:
            return None

        year = int(ym[:4])
        month = int(ym[4:6])
        nth = int(f_part)

        return _nth_weekday_of_month(
            year=year,
            month=month,
            weekday=4,  # Friday
            nth=nth,
        )

    except Exception:
        return None


def _get_weekly_friday_option_contract_date(
    trading_date,
    call_put: str = "call",
) -> str | None:
    """
    找出交易日當天要使用的週五選 contract_date。

    規則：
    1. 查當天 fm_taiwan_option_daily 內所有 F 合約
    2. 將 contract_date 轉成實際週五到期日
    3. 找第一個 到期日 > trading_date 的 contract_date
    """
    d = pd.Timestamp(trading_date).normalize()
    d_str = _dstr(d)

    cp = str(call_put).strip().lower()

    df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT DISTINCT contract_date
        FROM fm_taiwan_option_daily
        WHERE date = ?
          AND trading_session = 'position'
          AND LOWER(call_put) = ?
          AND contract_date LIKE '%F%'
        ORDER BY contract_date
        """,
        (d_str, cp),
    )

    if df is None or df.empty:
        return None

    candidates = []

    for contract_date in df["contract_date"].tolist():
        expiry_date = _parse_weekly_friday_option_contract_date(contract_date)

        if expiry_date is None:
            continue

        if expiry_date > d:
            candidates.append(
                (
                    expiry_date,
                    str(contract_date),
                )
            )

    if not candidates:
        return None

    candidates = sorted(candidates, key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))
    return candidates[0][1]


def _calc_weekly_friday_option_total_for_day(
    trading_date,
    call_put: str,
    option_id="TXO",
):
    """
    計算單日：
    - call_週五
    - put_週五

    使用：
    - 加權收盤價
    - 大台結算價
    - fm_taiwan_option_daily 的週五選 F 合約

    每筆 value：
    - 不乘 open_interest
    - value < 0 則改算 0
    """

    d = pd.Timestamp(trading_date).normalize()
    d_str = _dstr(d)

    cp = str(call_put).strip().lower()
    if cp not in ("call", "put"):
        raise ValueError("call_put 只能是 call 或 put")

    contract_date = _get_weekly_friday_option_contract_date(
        trading_date=d,
        call_put=cp,
    )

    if contract_date is None:
        print(f"[weekly_friday_option][skip] {d_str} cp={cp} 找不到週五選 contract_date")
        return None

    # =====================================================
    # 1. 讀取加權收盤價 / 大台結算價
    # =====================================================
    price_df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT
            加權收盤價,
            大台結算價
        FROM futures_report_daily_raw
        WHERE futures_id = 'TX'
          AND 日期 = ?
        LIMIT 1
        """,
        (d_str,),
    )

    if price_df is None or price_df.empty:
        print(f"[weekly_friday_option][skip] {d_str} futures_report_daily_raw 無價格資料")
        return None

    weighted_close = price_df.loc[0, "加權收盤價"]
    tx_settlement = price_df.loc[0, "大台結算價"]

    if pd.isna(weighted_close) or pd.isna(tx_settlement):
        print(f"[weekly_friday_option][skip] {d_str} 加權收盤價 / 大台結算價 為 NULL")
        return None

    # =====================================================
    # 2. 讀取週五選資料
    # =====================================================
    opt_df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT
            option_id,
            contract_date,
            strike_price,
            call_put,
            settlement_price,
            open_interest
        FROM fm_taiwan_option_daily
        WHERE date = ?
          AND trading_session = 'position'
          AND LOWER(call_put) = ?
          AND contract_date = ?
        ORDER BY contract_date, CAST(strike_price AS REAL)
        """,
        (d_str, cp, contract_date),
    )

    if opt_df is None or opt_df.empty:
        print(
            f"[weekly_friday_option][skip] {d_str} "
            f"cp={cp}, contract_date={contract_date}, 查無資料"
        )
        return None

    # =====================================================
    # 3. 逐筆計算，排除負值後加總
    # =====================================================
    total = 0.0
    has_value = False

    for _, row in opt_df.iterrows():
        row_value = _calc_option_row_value(
            call_put=row["call_put"],
            strike_price=row["strike_price"],
            settlement_price=row["settlement_price"],
            weighted_close=weighted_close,
            tx_base_price=tx_settlement,
        )

        if row_value is None or pd.isna(row_value):
            continue

        total += float(row_value)
        has_value = True

    if not has_value:
        return None

    return total


def _recalc_futures_report_daily_raw_weekly_friday_option(
    futures_id="TX",
    option_id="TXO",
    target_dates=None,
):
    """
    回填 futures_report_daily_raw：
    - call_週五
    - put_週五
    """

    fid = str(futures_id)

    if target_dates is None:
        df_dates = db.query_to_df(
            """
            SELECT 日期
            FROM futures_report_daily_raw
            WHERE futures_id = ?
            ORDER BY 日期
            """,
            (fid,),
        )

        if df_dates is None or df_dates.empty:
            return

        dates = pd.to_datetime(df_dates["日期"]).dt.normalize().tolist()

    else:
        dates = [
            pd.Timestamp(x).normalize()
            for x in target_dates
            if x is not None
        ]

    if not dates:
        return

    update_rows = []

    for d in dates:
        d_str = _dstr(d)

        call_total = _calc_weekly_friday_option_total_for_day(
            trading_date=d,
            call_put="call",
            option_id=option_id,
        )

        put_total = _calc_weekly_friday_option_total_for_day(
            trading_date=d,
            call_put="put",
            option_id=option_id,
        )

        update_rows.append(
            (
                call_total,
                put_total,
                fid,
                d_str,
            )
        )

    if not update_rows:
        return

    db.execute_sql(
        """
        UPDATE futures_report_daily_raw
        SET
            call_週五 = ?,
            put_週五 = ?
        WHERE futures_id = ?
          AND 日期 = ?
        """,
        update_rows,
    )

    print(f"[weekly_friday_option] updated={len(update_rows)}")

# =========================
# module/stock_report_utils.py
# =========================
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from common import db

TABLE = "stock_report_daily"

def get_db_columns():
    cols_df = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT column_name
        FROM information_schema.columns
        WHERE table_schema = ?
          AND table_name = ?
        ORDER BY ordinal_position
        """,
        ("public", TABLE),
    )

    if cols_df is None or cols_df.empty:
        raise RuntimeError(f"找不到資料表欄位：{TABLE}")

    return cols_df["column_name"].tolist()

def get_table_columns():
    """
    以 DB 真實欄位為準，不再手寫 COLUMNS。
    排除不應由 upsert 寫入的系統欄位。
    """
    skip_cols = {
        "id",
        "created_at",
        "updated_at",
    }

    return [
        c for c in get_db_columns()
        if c not in skip_cols
    ]

_FIELD_REGISTRY = None

def get_field_registry():
    global _FIELD_REGISTRY

    if _FIELD_REGISTRY is None:
        _FIELD_REGISTRY = set(get_table_columns())

    return _FIELD_REGISTRY


# =========================================================
# 0) 小工具
# =========================================================
def _is_missing(v):
    if v is None:
        return True
    if isinstance(v, float) and np.isnan(v):
        return True
    if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip() == "":
        return True
    try:
        if pd.isna(v):
            return True
    except Exception:
        pass
    return False


def _scol(df_, col, default=0.0):
    if col in df_.columns:
        s = df_[col]
        if isinstance(s, pd.Series):
            return s
        return pd.Series([s] * len(df_), index=df_.index)
    return pd.Series([default] * len(df_), index=df_.index)


def _sum_cols(df_, cols, default=0.0):
    out = pd.Series([0.0] * len(df_), index=df_.index, dtype="float64")
    hit = False
    for c in cols:
        if c in df_.columns:
            out = out + pd.to_numeric(_scol(df_, c, default=0.0), errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
            hit = True
    if not hit:
        return pd.Series([default] * len(df_), index=df_.index, dtype="float64")
    return out


def _sum_cols_like(df_, keywords, default=0.0):
    cols = []
    for c in df_.columns:
        cs = str(c)
        if any(k in cs for k in keywords):
            cols.append(c)
    if not cols:
        return pd.Series([default] * len(df_), index=df_.index, dtype="float64")
    return _sum_cols(df_, cols, default=default)


def classify_k_type(r):
    body = r.get("實體_pct", np.nan)
    upper = r.get("上影_pct", np.nan)
    lower = r.get("下影_pct", np.nan)
    open_p = r.get("開盤價", np.nan)
    close_p = r.get("收盤價", np.nan)

    if pd.isna(body) or pd.isna(upper) or pd.isna(lower):
        return None
    if pd.isna(open_p) or pd.isna(close_p):
        return None

    if abs(close_p - open_p) < 1e-6 or body < 0.05:
        return "⬜ 十字線"

    is_red = close_p > open_p
    color = "🟥" if is_red else "🟩"

    if lower > 0.5 and body < 0.3:
        return f"{color} 錘子線"
    if upper > 0.5 and body < 0.3:
        return f"{color} 流星線"
    if body > 0.6:
        return f"{color} 長紅K" if is_red else f"{color} 長黑K"
    return f"{color} 中實體K"


# =========================================================
# 1) 價量邏輯
# =========================================================
def _trend_regime(price_vs_ma):
    """
    依價格相對均線的位置，粗分多頭/空頭/中性
    > 0 : 多頭行情
    < 0 : 空頭行情
    """
    if pd.isna(price_vs_ma):
        return None
    if price_vs_ma > 0:
        return "多頭行情"
    if price_vs_ma < 0:
        return "空頭行情"
    return "中性"


def _pv_div_by_regime(regime, price_change, vol_change):
    if pd.isna(price_change) or pd.isna(vol_change) or regime is None:
        return None

    p_up = price_change > 0
    p_dn = price_change < 0
    v_up = vol_change > 0
    v_dn = vol_change < 0
    
    final_str = ""
    
    if p_up:
        final_str = final_str + "價漲"
    elif p_dn:
        final_str = final_str + "價跌"
    else:
        final_str = final_str + "價同"
        
    if v_up:
        final_str = final_str + "量增"
    elif v_dn:
        final_str = final_str + "量減"
    else:
        final_str = final_str + "量同"
        
    # if regime == "多頭行情":
    #     final_str = final_str + "(多頭)"
    # else:
    #     final_str = final_str + "(空頭)"
        
    """
    多頭行情：
      價漲量增 -> 多方
      價漲量減 -> 空方背離
      價跌量增 -> 多方背離
      價跌量減 -> 空方

    空頭行情：
      價漲量增 -> 空方背離
      價漲量減 -> 多方
      價跌量增 -> 多方背離
      價跌量減 -> 空方
    """

    # if regime == "多頭行情":
    #     if p_up and v_up:
    #         return "多方"
    #     if p_up and v_dn:
    #         return "空方背離"
    #     if p_dn and v_up:
    #         return "多方背離"
    #     if p_dn and v_dn:
    #         return "空方"

    # if regime == "空頭行情":
    #     if p_up and v_up:
    #         return "空方背離"
    #     if p_up and v_dn:
    #         return "多方"
    #     if p_dn and v_up:
    #         return "多方背離"
    #     if p_dn and v_dn:
    #         return "空方"

    # return None
    
    return final_str

def _fund_flow_text(price_bucket, fund_bucket):
    """
    規則：
      漲跌幅級距 == 法人級距 -> 中性
      法人級距 > 漲跌幅級距 -> 偏向大型股
      法人級距 < 漲跌幅級距 -> 偏向小型股
    """
    if pd.isna(price_bucket) or pd.isna(fund_bucket):
        return None, None

    price_bucket = int(price_bucket)
    fund_bucket = int(fund_bucket)

    if fund_bucket == price_bucket:
        label = "中性"
    elif fund_bucket > price_bucket:
        label = "偏向大型股"
    else:
        label = "偏向小型股"

    detail = f"{label}(價格:資金 = {price_bucket:+d}:{fund_bucket:+d})"
    return label, detail


# =========================================================
# 3) 重新抓可重建報告所需原始資料
# =========================================================
def _build_base_df(finMind, stock_id, sDt, eDt):
    """
    回傳以 date(datetime) 為主鍵、包含價格 + 法人(淨額) + 融資(今日餘額) 的 base df
    """
    df = finMind.get_tw_stock_daily_price(stock_id=stock_id, start_date=sDt, end_date=eDt)
    print(df.tail())
    if df is None or df.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame()

    df = df.copy()
    df["date"] = pd.to_datetime(df["date"])
    df = df[df["date"] <= pd.to_datetime(eDt.date())].reset_index(drop=True)

    # 法人
    df3 = finMind.get_tw_institutional_total(start_date=sDt, end_date=eDt)
    if df3 is None or df3.empty:
        df3 = pd.DataFrame(columns=["buy", "sell", "date", "name"])
    else:
        df3 = df3.copy()
        df3["net"] = pd.to_numeric(df3.get("buy"), errors="coerce") - pd.to_numeric(df3.get("sell"), errors="coerce")
        df3["date"] = pd.to_datetime(df3["date"])
        df3 = df3.pivot(index="date", columns="name", values="net").reset_index()

    df = df.merge(df3, on="date", how="left")

    # 融資：今日餘額（仟元）
    df["今日餘額"] = np.nan
    df_m = finMind.get_tw_margin_total(start_date=sDt, end_date=eDt)

    if df_m is not None and not df_m.empty:
        df_m = df_m.copy()
        col_date = "date" if "date" in df_m.columns else ("日期" if "日期" in df_m.columns else None)
        col_name = "name" if "name" in df_m.columns else ("項目" if "項目" in df_m.columns else None)
        col_today = "TodayBalance" if "TodayBalance" in df_m.columns else ("今日餘額" if "今日餘額" in df_m.columns else None)

        if col_date and col_name and col_today:
            df_m[col_date] = pd.to_datetime(df_m[col_date])
            df_m[col_name] = df_m[col_name].astype(str)

            cand = df_m[df_m[col_name] == "MarginPurchaseMoney"].copy()
            if not cand.empty:
                cand[col_today] = pd.to_numeric(cand[col_today], errors="coerce")
                med = cand[col_today].median(skipna=True)

                # 太大視為元，轉成仟元
                if pd.notna(med) and med > 1e9:
                    cand["今日餘額"] = cand[col_today] / 1000.0
                else:
                    cand["今日餘額"] = cand[col_today]

                cand = cand[[col_date, "今日餘額"]].rename(columns={col_date: "date"})
                df = df.merge(cand, on="date", how="left", suffixes=("", "_m"))

                if "今日餘額_m" in df.columns:
                    df["今日餘額"] = df["今日餘額"].combine_first(df["今日餘額_m"])
                    df.drop(columns=["今日餘額_m"], inplace=True, errors="ignore")

    return df


# =========================================================
# 4) 計算衍生欄
# =========================================================
def _compute_derived(df):
    df = df.copy()
    df = df.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)

    df.rename(
        columns={
            "date": "日期",
            "open": "開盤價",
            "close": "收盤價",
            "max": "最高價",
            "min": "最低價",
            "Trading_Volume": "成交量",
            "Trading_money": "總成交金額_億",
        },
        inplace=True,
    )

    # =========================
    # 基本數值轉型
    # =========================
    for c in ["開盤價", "收盤價", "最高價", "最低價", "成交量", "總成交金額_億"]:
        if c in df.columns:
            df[c] = pd.to_numeric(df[c], errors="coerce")

    prev_close = df["收盤價"].shift(1)
    denom_open = df["開盤價"].replace(0, np.nan)

    # =========================
    # 基本價量
    # =========================
    df["收盤_開盤"] = df["收盤價"] - df["開盤價"]
    df["日振幅"] = df["最高價"] - df["最低價"]
    df["漲跌"] = df["收盤價"] - prev_close
    df["漲跌幅_pct"] = (df["收盤價"] - prev_close) / prev_close
    df["量增率_pct"] = (df["成交量"] - df["成交量"].shift(1)) / df["成交量"].shift(1)

    base_range = df["日振幅"] / prev_close.replace(0, np.nan)
    sign = np.sign(df["收盤價"] - prev_close)
    df["日振幅_昨收_pct"] = base_range * sign
    df["日振福_開盤_pct"] = (df["收盤價"] - df["開盤價"]) / denom_open
    df["日振幅/開盤"] = df["日振幅"] / denom_open

    # =========================
    # 年 / 月 / 星期 / 週次 / 天別
    # =========================
    dt = pd.to_datetime(df["日期"])
    df["年"] = dt.dt.year
    df["月"] = dt.dt.month

    wd_map = {
        0: "星期一", 1: "星期二", 2: "星期三", 3: "星期四",
        4: "星期五", 5: "星期六", 6: "星期日"
    }
    df["星期"] = dt.dt.weekday.map(wd_map)

    y = dt.dt.year
    jan1 = pd.to_datetime(y.astype(str) + "-01-01")
    jan1_wd = jan1.dt.weekday
    offset = (7 - jan1_wd) % 7
    firstMon = jan1 + pd.to_timedelta(offset, unit="D")
    week = ((dt - firstMon).dt.days // 7) + 1
    df["週次"] = week.clip(lower=1).astype(int)

    def _day_bucket(wd):
        if wd == 0:
            return "頭"
        if wd in (1, 2, 3):
            return "週間"
        if wd == 4:
            return "尾"
        return "週間"

    df["天別"] = dt.dt.weekday.apply(_day_bucket)

    # =========================
    # 均量 / 均價 / 扣抵 / 乖離 / 價量
    # =========================
    for n in [5, 10, 20, 60]:
        df[f"{n}日均量"] = df["成交量"].rolling(n).mean()
        df[f"{n}MA"] = df["收盤價"].rolling(n).mean()
        # df[f"{n}日上升幅度"] = df[f"{n}MA"] - df[f"{n}MA"].shift(1)
        # df[f"{n}日扣抵值"] = df["收盤價"].shift(n - 1)
        # df[f"{n}日扣抵影響_pct"] = (df["收盤價"] - df[f"{n}日扣抵值"]) / df["收盤價"].replace(0, np.nan)
        df[f"{n}日乖離"] = (df["收盤價"] - df[f"{n}MA"]) / df[f"{n}MA"].replace(0, np.nan)

        denom_volma = df[f"{n}日均量"].replace(0, np.nan)
        df[f"{n}日量增率_pct"] = (df["成交量"] - df[f"{n}日均量"]) / denom_volma

        df[f"{n}日行情"] = df[f"{n}日乖離"].apply(_trend_regime)

        df[f"{n}日價量"] = [
            _pv_div_by_regime(regime, pchg, vchg)
            for regime, pchg, vchg in zip(
                df[f"{n}日行情"],
                df[f"{n}日乖離"],
                df[f"{n}日量增率_pct"],
            )
        ]

    # =========================
    # 當日價量
    # =========================
    df["當日行情"] = ((df["收盤價"] - df["5MA"]) / df["5MA"].replace(0, np.nan)).apply(_trend_regime)

    df["當日價量"] = [
        _pv_div_by_regime(regime, pchg, vchg)
        for regime, pchg, vchg in zip(
            df["當日行情"],
            df["漲跌幅_pct"],
            df["量增率_pct"],
        )
    ]

    # =========================
    # 金額換算
    # =========================
    df["總成交金額_億"] = pd.to_numeric(df["總成交金額_億"], errors="coerce") / 1e8

    # =========================
    # 法人
    # =========================
    foreign = pd.to_numeric(_scol(df, "Foreign_Investor", default=np.nan), errors="coerce")
    itrust = pd.to_numeric(_scol(df, "Investment_Trust", default=np.nan), errors="coerce")
    dealer = _sum_cols_like(df, keywords=["Dealer"], default=0.0)

    total_net = pd.to_numeric(_scol(df, "total", default=np.nan), errors="coerce")
    if total_net.isna().all():
        total_net = (
            foreign.fillna(0.0) +
            itrust.fillna(0.0) +
            pd.to_numeric(dealer, errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        )

    df["法人總買超_億"] = total_net / 1e8
    df["買超_外資_億"] = foreign / 1e8
    df["買超_投信_億"] = itrust / 1e8
    df["買超_自營商_億"] = pd.to_numeric(dealer, errors="coerce").fillna(0.0) / 1e8

    # =========================
    # 融資（今日餘額：仟元）
    # =========================
    df["融資餘額_億"] = pd.to_numeric(_scol(df, "今日餘額", default=np.nan), errors="coerce") * 1000 / 1e8
    df["買超_融資_億"] = df["融資餘額_億"] - df["融資餘額_億"].shift(1)
    df["法人淨買賣超_扣融資_億"] = df["法人總買超_億"] - df["買超_融資_億"]

    # =========================
    # 法人買賣超 / 總成交 比例（扣融資後）
    # =========================
    denom = df["總成交金額_億"].replace(0, np.nan)
    df["法人買賣超_總成交_pct"] = (
        (df["法人總買超_億"] - df["買超_融資_億"]) / denom
    )

    # =========================
    # K 棒影線
    # =========================
    rng = (df["最高價"] - df["最低價"]).replace(0, np.nan)
    df["實體_pct"] = (df["收盤價"] - df["開盤價"]).abs() / rng
    df["上影_pct"] = (df["最高價"] - np.maximum(df["開盤價"], df["收盤價"])) / rng
    df["下影_pct"] = (np.minimum(df["開盤價"], df["收盤價"]) - df["最低價"]) / rng
    df["K線型態"] = df.apply(classify_k_type, axis=1)

    # =========================
    # 跳空缺口 / 跳空缺口_昨收_pct
    # =========================
    df["昨高"] = df["最高價"].shift(1)
    df["昨低"] = df["最低價"].shift(1)
    df["跳空狀態"] = np.select(
        [df["最低價"] > df["昨高"], df["最高價"] < df["昨低"]],
        ["上跳空", "下跳空"],
        default="無跳空",
    )

    is_red = df["收盤價"] > df["開盤價"]
    df["今上緣"] = np.where(is_red, df["收盤價"], df["開盤價"])
    df["今下緣"] = np.where(is_red, df["開盤價"], df["收盤價"])
    df["昨上緣"] = df["今上緣"].shift(1)
    df["昨下緣"] = df["今下緣"].shift(1)

    df["跳空缺口"] = np.select(
        [df["跳空狀態"] == "上跳空", df["跳空狀態"] == "下跳空"],
        [df["今下緣"] - df["昨上緣"], df["今上緣"] - df["昨下緣"]],
        default=np.nan,
    )

    df["跳空缺口_昨收_pct"] = df["跳空缺口"] / prev_close.replace(0, np.nan)

    # =========================
    # 最大量
    # =========================
    vols = pd.to_numeric(df["成交量"], errors="coerce").fillna(0).to_numpy()
    dates_arr = pd.to_datetime(df["日期"]).dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d").to_numpy()

    for n in [5, 10, 20, 60]:
        vmax_list, vdate_list = [], []
        for i in range(len(df)):
            if i + 1 < n:
                vmax_list.append(np.nan)
                vdate_list.append(np.nan)
                continue

            window_vol = vols[i + 1 - n:i + 1]
            window_date = dates_arr[i + 1 - n:i + 1]
            idx = int(np.argmax(window_vol))
            vmax_list.append(window_vol[idx])
            vdate_list.append(window_date[idx])

        df[f"{n}日最大量"] = vmax_list
        df[f"{n}日最大量_日期"] = vdate_list

    # ===== [補] 價格漲跌幅 =====
    df["漲跌幅_pct"] = np.where(
        df["收盤價"].shift(1) > 0,
        (df["收盤價"] - df["收盤價"].shift(1)) / df["收盤價"].shift(1) * 100,
        np.nan
    )
    
    # =========================
    # 🔥 百分位 / 級距（修正後）
    # 規則：
    # - 不往前多抓
    # - 不夠一年跨度就留 NULL
    # - 正負分開
    # =========================
    df["漲跌幅百分位數"] = _calc_signed_percentile_by_date(
        dates=df["日期"],
        series=df["漲跌幅_pct"],
        window_days=365,
        min_samples=20,
    )

    df["三大法人買賣超百分位數"] = _calc_signed_percentile_by_date(
        dates=df["日期"],
        series=df["法人淨買賣超_扣融資_億"],   # ← 改這行
        window_days=365,
        min_samples=20,
    )
    
    

    df["漲跌幅級距"] = df["漲跌幅百分位數"].apply(_to_bucket)
    df["三大法人買賣超級距"] = df["三大法人買賣超百分位數"].apply(_to_bucket)
    
    # =========================
    # 🔥 資金走向判定（核心）
    # =========================
    fund_labels = [
        _fund_flow_text(p, f)[0]
        for p, f in zip(df["漲跌幅級距"], df["三大法人買賣超級距"])
    ]

    df["資金走向判定"] = fund_labels

    # =========================
    # 不存中間計算欄
    # =========================
    df.drop(
        columns=[
            "當日行情",
            "5日行情", "10日行情", "20日行情", "60日行情",
            "融資餘額_億",
            "昨高", "昨低", "跳空狀態", "今上緣", "今下緣", "昨上緣", "昨下緣",
        ],
        inplace=True,
        errors="ignore",
    )

    return df

# =========================================================
# 5) update_is_complete
# =========================================================
def update_is_complete():
    exclude_cols = {
        "is_complete",
        "id",
        "created_at",
        "updated_at",
    }

    all_cols = get_db_columns()
    check_cols = [c for c in all_cols if c not in exclude_cols]

    if not check_cols:
        print("⚠ 沒有可檢查欄位，跳過更新。")
        return

    def q(name: str) -> str:
        return '"' + name.replace('"', '""') + '"'

    null_conditions = " OR ".join([f"{q(c)} IS NULL" for c in check_cols])

    sql = f"""
    UPDATE {q(TABLE)}
    SET {q("is_complete")} = CASE
        WHEN {null_conditions} THEN 0
        ELSE 1
    END;
    """
    print("執行 SQL 中 ...")
    ok = db.execute_sql(sql)
    print("更新完成 ✔" if ok else "更新失敗 ❌")


# =========================================================
# 6) UPSERT
# =========================================================
def upsert(df: pd.DataFrame, stock_id: str):
    if df is None or df.empty:
        print("⚠ df empty, skip")
        return

    df = df.copy()
    df["股票代號"] = stock_id
    df["日期"] = pd.to_datetime(df["日期"]).dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    db_columns = get_db_columns()
    
    skip_cols = {"id", "created_at", "updated_at"}

    # 只寫 df 真的有的欄位，不要把 DB 欄位全部補 NaN
    write_columns = [
        c for c in db_columns
        if c not in skip_cols and c in df.columns
    ]

    # 確保主鍵一定在
    for c in ["股票代號", "日期"]:
        if c not in write_columns:
            write_columns.insert(0, c)

    # 不在這裡算完整度，交給 update_is_complete()
    if "is_complete" in db_columns and "is_complete" not in write_columns:
        df["is_complete"] = 0
        write_columns.append("is_complete")

    df = df[write_columns]

    print("準備寫入日期範圍：", df["日期"].min(), "~", df["日期"].max())
    print(df[["日期", "股票代號", "收盤價"]].tail())

    col_sql = ",".join([f'"{c}"' for c in write_columns])
    ph = ",".join(["?"] * len(write_columns))

    update_cols = [
        c for c in write_columns
        if c not in ("日期", "股票代號")
    ]

    update_sql = ",".join([
        f'"{c}" = excluded."{c}"'
        for c in update_cols
    ])

    sql = f"""
    INSERT INTO "{TABLE}" ({col_sql})
    VALUES ({ph})
    ON CONFLICT("股票代號","日期") DO UPDATE SET
        {update_sql};
    """

    rows = list(df.itertuples(index=False, name=None))

    ok = db.execute_sql(sql, rows)

    if ok:
        print(f"✔ DB 寫入成功: {len(df)} rows, stock={stock_id}")

        check = db.query_to_df(f"""
            SELECT MAX(日期) AS max_date
            FROM "{TABLE}"
            WHERE 股票代號 = '{stock_id}'
        """)
        print("DB 最新日期：")
        print(check)
    else:
        print("❌ DB 寫入失敗（請看上方 PostgreSQL Error）")


# =========================================================
# 7) repair 用：只更新指定欄位
# =========================================================
def _to_sqlite_value(v):
    if v is None:
        return None
    try:
        if pd.isna(v):
            return None
    except Exception:
        pass
    if isinstance(v, pd.Timestamp):
        return v.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    if isinstance(v, np.generic):
        return v.item()
    return v


def _update_fields_to_db(key_cols, df_patch, fields):
    if df_patch is None or df_patch.empty:
        return 0

    df_patch = df_patch.copy()
    if "日期" in df_patch.columns:
        df_patch["日期"] = pd.to_datetime(df_patch["日期"]).dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    set_clause = ", ".join([f'"{c}" = ?' for c in fields])
    where_clause = " AND ".join([f'"{k}" = ?' for k in key_cols])
    sql = f'UPDATE "{TABLE}" SET {set_clause} WHERE {where_clause}'

    params = []
    for _, r in df_patch.iterrows():
        row_vals = (
            [_to_sqlite_value(r.get(c, None)) for c in fields] +
            [_to_sqlite_value(r.get(k, None)) for k in key_cols]
        )
        params.append(tuple(row_vals))

    if hasattr(db, "executemany") and callable(getattr(db, "executemany")):
        db.executemany(sql, params)
    else:
        for p in params:
            db.execute_sql(sql, p)

    return len(params)


# =========================================================
# 🔥 百分位計算（核心）
# =========================================================

def _calc_signed_percentile_by_date(dates, series, window_days=365, min_samples=20):
    """
    規則：
    1. 只用目前資料集內，且在「當前日期之前」的資料
    2. 需要至少滿 365 天歷史跨度，否則直接 NaN
    3. 正負分開算
    4. 同方向樣本數 < min_samples -> NaN
    5. 不夠資料時，x==0 也不特判，照樣 NaN
    """
    dt = pd.to_datetime(dates)
    s = pd.to_numeric(series, errors="coerce")

    out = np.full(len(s), np.nan, dtype="float64")

    for i in range(len(s)):
        x = s.iloc[i]
        if pd.isna(x):
            continue

        cur_dt = dt.iloc[i]

        # ❗一定要滿365天歷史跨度，不夠就不要算
        earliest_allowed = cur_dt - pd.Timedelta(days=window_days)
        if dt.iloc[0] > earliest_allowed:
            continue

        # 只取目前資料內、當前日期之前、且落在前365天內的資料
        hist_mask = (dt >= earliest_allowed) & (dt < cur_dt)
        hist = s.loc[hist_mask].dropna()

        # 不把 0 混進正負樣本
        hist = hist[hist != 0]

        if x > 0:
            sample = hist[hist > 0]
        elif x < 0:
            sample = hist[hist < 0]
        else:
            # 不夠資料就不要硬給0
            continue

        if len(sample) < min_samples:
            continue

        rank = (sample < x).sum() + 1
        pct = rank / len(sample)

        out[i] = pct if x > 0 else -pct

    return pd.Series(out, index=series.index)


def _to_bucket(x):
    if pd.isna(x):
        return np.nan

    if x >= 0.9:
        return 3
    elif x >= 0.7:
        return 2
    elif x >= 0.3:
        return 1
    elif x > -0.3:
        return 0
    elif x > -0.7:
        return -1
    elif x > -0.9:
        return -2
    else:
        return -3

import re
import time
import requests
import pandas as pd
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Tuple, List
from common import db  # 你原本的 DB 模組

# ============================================================
# helpers
# ============================================================

def _dstr(ts: pd.Timestamp) -> str:
    return ts.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")


def _to_unix_ts(date_yyyy_mm_dd: str) -> int:
    # date string -> unix seconds (00:00:00 local)
    dt = pd.Timestamp(date_yyyy_mm_dd).to_pydatetime()
    return int(time.mktime(dt.timetuple()))


def _clean_float(x) -> Optional[float]:
    if x is None:
        return None
    s = str(x).strip()
    if s == "" or s.upper() in {"N/A", "NA", "NULL"} or s in {"-----", "--", "-"}:
        return None
    s = s.replace(",", "")
    # 有些是 "18.90     " 這種
    try:
        return float(s)
    except Exception:
        return None


def _clean_int(x) -> Optional[int]:
    if x is None:
        return None
    s = str(x).strip()
    if s == "" or s.upper() in {"N/A", "NA", "NULL"} or s in {"-----", "--", "-"}:
        return None
    s = s.replace(",", "")
    try:
        return int(float(s))
    except Exception:
        return None


def _roc_to_ad_yyyy_mm_dd(s: str) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    支援：
    - "115/01/15"
    - "100.01.03"
    - "114/12/31"
    - "20110103" / "2026/01/15" / "2026-01-15"（若本來就是西元也盡量吃）
    回傳 "YYYY-MM-DD"
    """
    if s is None:
        return None
    s = str(s).strip()
    if not s:
        return None

    # 先抓純數字 YYYYMMDD
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d{8})", s)
    if m:
        y = int(s[:4])
        mm = int(s[4:6])
        dd = int(s[6:8])
        return f"{y:04d}-{mm:02d}-{dd:02d}"

    # 支援 YYYY/MM/DD 或 YYYY-MM-DD
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d{4})[\/\-\.](\d{1,2})[\/\-\.](\d{1,2})", s)
    if m:
        y = int(m.group(1))
        mm = int(m.group(2))
        dd = int(m.group(3))
        # 若 y 很小(<=1911) 才可能是民國？一般不會，這裡當西元
        return f"{y:04d}-{mm:02d}-{dd:02d}"

    # 民國 "115/01/15" 或 "100.01.03"
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d{2,3})[\/\.](\d{1,2})[\/\.](\d{1,2})", s)
    if m:
        roc_y = int(m.group(1))
        y = roc_y + 1911
        mm = int(m.group(2))
        dd = int(m.group(3))
        return f"{y:04d}-{mm:02d}-{dd:02d}"

    return None


def _parse_range_roc(s: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str], Optional[int]]:
    """
    解析 "115/01/16～115/01/29" 或 "115/01/16~115/01/29" 或 "115/01/16～115/01/29"
    回傳 (start_yyyy_mm_dd, end_yyyy_mm_dd, total_days_inclusive_or_none)
    total_days 以「日曆日含頭尾」粗算（不是交易日）
    """
    if s is None:
        return (None, None, None)
    t = str(s).strip()
    if not t:
        return (None, None, None)

    # 各種分隔符
    t = t.replace("～", "~").replace("—", "-").replace("–", "-")
    parts = t.split("~")
    if len(parts) != 2:
        return (None, None, None)

    s1 = _roc_to_ad_yyyy_mm_dd(parts[0].strip())
    s2 = _roc_to_ad_yyyy_mm_dd(parts[1].strip())
    if not s1 or not s2:
        return (s1, s2, None)

    try:
        d1 = pd.Timestamp(s1)
        d2 = pd.Timestamp(s2)
        total = int((d2 - d1).days) + 1
    except Exception:
        total = None
    return (s1, s2, total)


def _get_span(target_table: str) -> Tuple[Optional[pd.Timestamp], Optional[pd.Timestamp]]:
    span_row = db.query_to_df(
        """
        SELECT start_date, end_date
        FROM date_span
        WHERE target_table = ? AND idx_key = ?
        """,
        (target_table, "date"),
    )
    if span_row is None or span_row.empty:
        return (None, None)
    mem_s = pd.Timestamp(span_row.loc[0, "start_date"]).normalize()
    mem_e = pd.Timestamp(span_row.loc[0, "end_date"]).normalize()
    return (mem_s, mem_e)


def _calc_fetch_ranges(
    req_s: pd.Timestamp,
    req_e: pd.Timestamp,
    mem_s: Optional[pd.Timestamp],
    mem_e: Optional[pd.Timestamp],
) -> Tuple[List[Tuple[pd.Timestamp, pd.Timestamp]], pd.Timestamp, pd.Timestamp]:
    fetch_ranges: List[Tuple[pd.Timestamp, pd.Timestamp]] = []

    if mem_s is None or mem_e is None:
        return [(req_s, req_e)], req_s, req_e

    new_s = min(mem_s, req_s)
    new_e = max(mem_e, req_e)

    if req_s >= mem_s and req_e <= mem_e:
        fetch_ranges = []
    elif req_s > mem_e:
        fetch_ranges = [(mem_e + pd.Timedelta(days=1), req_e)]
    elif req_e < mem_s:
        fetch_ranges = [(req_s, mem_s - pd.Timedelta(days=1))]
    else:
        if req_s < mem_s:
            fetch_ranges.append((req_s, mem_s - pd.Timedelta(days=1)))
        if req_e > mem_e:
            fetch_ranges.append((mem_e + pd.Timedelta(days=1), req_e))

    return fetch_ranges, new_s, new_e


def _update_span(target_table: str, new_s: pd.Timestamp, new_e: pd.Timestamp) -> None:
    db.execute_sql(
        """
        INSERT INTO date_span (target_table, idx_key, start_date, end_date, updated_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
        ON CONFLICT(target_table, idx_key) DO UPDATE SET
          start_date = excluded.start_date,
          end_date   = excluded.end_date,
          updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        """,
        (target_table, "date", _dstr(new_s), _dstr(new_e)),
    )


# ============================================================
# 3) TPEX attention (上櫃公布注意有價證券)
# ============================================================

def get_tpex_bulletin_attention(start_date: datetime, end_date: datetime) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    對應：
      POST https://www.tpex.org.tw/www/zh-tw/bulletin/attention
      payload: startDate=YYYY/MM/DD&endDate=YYYY/MM/DD&...&response=json

    入庫表：tpex_bulletin_attention
    快取：date_span(target_table='tpex_bulletin_attention', idx_key='date')
    """
    target_table = "tpex_bulletin_attention"
    req_s = pd.Timestamp(start_date).normalize()
    req_e = pd.Timestamp(end_date).normalize()
    if req_s > req_e:
        raise ValueError("start_date 不可大於 end_date")

    mem_s, mem_e = _get_span(target_table)
    fetch_ranges, new_s, new_e = _calc_fetch_ranges(req_s, req_e, mem_s, mem_e)

    url = "https://www.tpex.org.tw/www/zh-tw/bulletin/attention"

    delete_sql = f"DELETE FROM {target_table} WHERE 公告日期 >= ? AND 公告日期 <= ?"

    insert_sql = f"""
    INSERT INTO {target_table}
      (證券代號, 證券名稱, 累計, 注意交易資訊, 公告日期, 收盤價, 本益比, link, created_at, 公告日期_ts)
    VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
    """

    for fs, fe in fetch_ranges:
        if fs > fe:
            continue

        payload = {
            "startDate": fs.strftime("%Y/%m/%d"),
            "endDate": fe.strftime("%Y/%m/%d"),
            "code": "",
            "cate": "",
            "type": "all",
            "order": "date",
            "id": "",
            "response": "json",
        }

        r = requests.post(url, data=payload, timeout=30)
        r.raise_for_status()
        js = r.json()

        tables = js.get("tables") or []
        if not tables:
            continue

        data = tables[0].get("data") or []
        if not data:
            continue

        rows = []
        for item in data:
            # fields:
            # ["編號","證券代號","證券名稱","累計","注意交易資訊","公告日期","收盤價","本益比","link"]
            sid = str(item[1]).strip()
            name = str(item[2]).strip()
            acc = _clean_int(item[3])
            info = str(item[4]).strip()
            pub_roc = str(item[5]).strip()  # 115/01/15
            pub_ad = _roc_to_ad_yyyy_mm_dd(pub_roc)
            close_p = _clean_float(item[6])
            pe = _clean_float(item[7])
            link = str(item[8]).strip() if item[8] is not None else None

            if not pub_ad:
                continue

            rows.append((
                sid, name, acc, info, pub_ad, close_p, pe, link,
                pd.Timestamp.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                _to_unix_ts(pub_ad),
            ))

        if rows:
            db.execute_sql(delete_sql, (_dstr(fs), _dstr(fe)))
            db.execute_sql(insert_sql, rows)

    _update_span(target_table, new_s, new_e)

    df = db.query_to_df(
        f"""
        SELECT id, 證券代號, 證券名稱, 累計, 注意交易資訊, 公告日期, 收盤價, 本益比, link, created_at, 公告日期_ts
        FROM {target_table}
        WHERE 公告日期 >= ? AND 公告日期 <= ?
        ORDER BY 公告日期, 證券代號
        """,
        (_dstr(req_s), _dstr(req_e)),
    )
    return df if df is not None else pd.DataFrame()


# ============================================================
# 4) TPEX disposal (上櫃公布處置有價證券資訊)
# ============================================================

def get_tpex_bulletin_disposal(start_date: datetime, end_date: datetime) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    對應：
      POST https://www.tpex.org.tw/www/zh-tw/bulletin/disposal
      payload: startDate=YYYY/MM/DD&endDate=YYYY/MM/DD&...&response=json

    入庫表：tpex_bulletin_disposal（UNIQUE(證券代號, 公布日期)）
    快取：date_span(target_table='tpex_bulletin_disposal', idx_key='date')
    """
    target_table = "tpex_bulletin_disposal"
    req_s = pd.Timestamp(start_date).normalize()
    req_e = pd.Timestamp(end_date).normalize()
    if req_s > req_e:
        raise ValueError("start_date 不可大於 end_date")

    mem_s, mem_e = _get_span(target_table)
    fetch_ranges, new_s, new_e = _calc_fetch_ranges(req_s, req_e, mem_s, mem_e)

    url = "https://www.tpex.org.tw/www/zh-tw/bulletin/disposal"

    upsert_sql = f"""
    INSERT INTO {target_table}
      (公布日期, 證券代號, 證券名稱, 累計, 處置起訖時間, 處置原因, 處置內容, 收盤價, 本益比, memo,
       created_at, 公布日期_ts,
       law_src, last_notice, notice_cnt, notice_dt,
       處置起始日, 處置結束日, 處置總天數)
    VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, ?,
            ?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
    ON CONFLICT(證券代號, 公布日期) DO UPDATE SET
      證券名稱 = excluded.證券名稱,
      累計 = excluded.累計,
      處置起訖時間 = excluded.處置起訖時間,
      處置原因 = excluded.處置原因,
      處置內容 = excluded.處置內容,
      收盤價 = excluded.收盤價,
      本益比 = excluded.本益比,
      memo = excluded.memo,
      公布日期_ts = excluded.公布日期_ts,
      law_src = excluded.law_src,
      last_notice = excluded.last_notice,
      notice_cnt = excluded.notice_cnt,
      notice_dt = excluded.notice_dt,
      處置起始日 = excluded.處置起始日,
      處置結束日 = excluded.處置結束日,
      處置總天數 = excluded.處置總天數,
      created_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    """

    for fs, fe in fetch_ranges:
        if fs > fe:
            continue

        payload = {
            "startDate": fs.strftime("%Y/%m/%d"),
            "endDate": fe.strftime("%Y/%m/%d"),
            "code": "",
            "cate": "",
            "type": "all",
            "reason": "-1",
            "measure": "-1",
            "order": "date",
            "id": "",
            "response": "json",
        }

        r = requests.post(url, data=payload, timeout=30)
        r.raise_for_status()
        js = r.json()

        tables = js.get("tables") or []
        if not tables:
            continue

        data = tables[0].get("data") or []
        if not data:
            continue

        rows = []
        for item in data:
            # fields:
            # ["編號","公布日期","證券代號","證券名稱","累計","處置起訖時間","處置原因","處置內容","收盤價","本益比"," "]
            pub_roc = str(item[1]).strip()
            pub_ad = _roc_to_ad_yyyy_mm_dd(pub_roc)
            sid = str(item[2]).strip()

            # 證券名稱有時含 "(../../mainboard/...)" 這種
            raw_name = str(item[3]).strip()
            name = raw_name.split("(")[0].strip()

            acc = _clean_int(item[4])
            period = str(item[5]).strip()
            reason = str(item[6]).strip()
            content = str(item[7]).strip()
            close_p = _clean_float(item[8])
            pe = _clean_float(item[9])
            memo = item[10]  # 多行字串/連結
            memo_str = str(memo) if memo is not None else None

            if not pub_ad:
                continue

            s_ad, e_ad, total_days = _parse_range_roc(period)

            law_src = None
            last_notice = None
            notice_cnt = None
            notice_dt = None

            rows.append((
                pub_ad, sid, name, acc, period, reason, content, close_p, pe, memo_str,
                _to_unix_ts(pub_ad),
                law_src, last_notice, notice_cnt, notice_dt,
                s_ad, e_ad, total_days
            ))

        if rows:
            db.execute_sql(upsert_sql, rows)

    _update_span(target_table, new_s, new_e)

    df = db.query_to_df(
        f"""
        SELECT *
        FROM {target_table}
        WHERE 公布日期 >= ? AND 公布日期 <= ?
        ORDER BY 公布日期, 證券代號
        """,
        (_dstr(req_s), _dstr(req_e)),
    )
    return df if df is not None else pd.DataFrame()

# ======== 4. 融資融券餘額 ========
# 項目,買進,賣出,現金(券)償還,前日餘額,今日餘額
def fetch_margin_trading(date: datetime | None = None):
    date_str = date.strftime("%Y%m%d")
    apiEndpoint = "marginTrading/MI_MARGN"
    apiParams = f"date={date_str}&selectType=MS&{common_params}"
    apiUrl = f"{twseUrl}/{apiEndpoint}?{apiParams}"

    try:
        res = requests.get(apiUrl, timeout=10)
        text = res.text.strip()

        # 1) 空白 → 假日 or TWSE 掛掉
        if text == "":
            print(f"[跳過] TWSE 回傳空白（假日?）: {date_str}")
            return None

        # 2) HTML → 被擋 or 維護
        if text.startswith("<") or text.startswith("<!--"):
            print(f"[跳過] TWSE 回傳 HTML（維護或被BAN）: {date_str}")
            return None

        # 3) 嘗試解析 JSON
        data = res.json()

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[錯誤] JSON 解析失敗 {date_str}: {e}")
        return None

    # 4) TWSE 自己給的錯誤訊息
    if "stat" in data and ("無" in data["stat"] or "抱歉" in data["stat"]):
        print(f"[跳過] 無融資資料: {date_str}")
        return None

    # 5) tables 結構不完整
    tables = data.get("tables")
    if not tables or "fields" not in tables[0] or "data" not in tables[0]:
        print(f"[跳過] TWSE 回傳格式錯誤: {date_str}")
        return None

    return tables[0]

# # ======== 4.2 融資融券餘額 區間版 ========
# # 日期,項目,買進,賣出,現金_券_償還,前日餘額,今日餘額
def fetch_margin_trading_range(sDt: datetime, eDt: datetime):
    if sDt > eDt:
        return None
    
    data = []    
    current = sDt

    while current <= eDt:
        currentData = fetch_margin_trading(current)

        # 假日 or 無資料 or HTML → 跳過這一天
        if currentData is None:
            current += timedelta(days=1)
            continue
        
        rows = currentData.get("data", [])
        date_str = current.strftime("%Y%m%d")

        for aRow in rows:
            aRow.insert(0, date_str)

        data.extend(rows)
        current += timedelta(days=1)
        
    return {
        "fields": ['日期', '項目', '買進', '賣出', '現金_券_償還', '前日餘額', '今日餘額'],
        "data": data
    }


# ======== 5. 注意股公告 ========
# 編號,證券代號,證券名稱,累計次數,注意交易資訊,日期,收盤價,本益比
def fetch_notice(sDt: datetime | None = None, eDt: datetime | None = None):
    if (sDt > eDt):
        return None

    if eDt > datetime.today():
        eDt = datetime.today()

    start_str = sDt.strftime("%Y%m%d")
    end_str = eDt.strftime("%Y%m%d")

    sortKinds = ["STKNO", "DATE"]

    apiEndpoint = "announcement/notice"
    apiParams = f"querytype=1&{common_params}"
    apiParams += f"&stockNo=&selectType=&sortKind={sortKinds[1]}"
    apiParams += f"&startDate={start_str}&endDate={end_str}"
    apiUrl = f"{twseUrl}/{apiEndpoint}?{apiParams}"

    res = requests.get(apiUrl)
    raw_data = res.json()
    df = pd.DataFrame(raw_data.get("data", []), columns=raw_data.get("fields", []))
    tools._save_to_csv(df, apiEndpoint, f"{start_str}_{end_str}")
    del df
    return raw_data

# ======== 範例測試 ========
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # test = datetime.today()
    test = datetime(2025, 9, 3)
    print(fetch_margin_trading_range(test, test))
